### [1] [Sparse tensor networks for individual tree diameter estimation in mixed forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37784)
*Clara Lößl,Fabian Ewald Fassnacht,Viacheslav Barkov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量稀疏张量网络TreeNext，直接从体素化ULS点云回归单木胸径，自动分割树R²=0.8355、真值分割R²=0.9380，优于RF/XGBoost基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全林分/区域人工测树不可行，ULS可规模化但冠层遮挡导致茎干点密度低，QSMs与圆拟合等依赖茎干重建的方法受限；预定义点云指标的机器学习不够充分，需直接从点云学习的方案。

Method: 在德国四块混交温带林样地，构建从单木实例分割到DBH回归的全自动流程；将点云体素化后输入轻量稀疏张量网络TreeNext进行逐树参数回归，并对比自动分割与真值分割以量化分割误差影响，同时与Random Forest、XGBoost等表格基线比较。

Result: TreeNext在自动分割树上R²=0.8355，真值分割上R²=0.9380，优于强表格基线；分割质量是自动流程中的主导误差源；模型数据效率高，小样本场景仍表现良好。

Conclusion: 稀疏张量网络可作为ULS单木DBH自动估计的实用基础；未来提升重点在于实例分割精度而非回归模型本身。

Abstract: Measurements of tree diameter at breast height (DBH) are fundamental to forest inventories, but manual surveys across entire stands or regions are impractical. UAV-borne laser scanning (ULS) enables scalable automated forest surveys, yet canopy occlusion makes accurate DBH estimation from ULS point clouds non-trivial. Limited stem-level point density of ULS data constrains approaches relying on detailed stem reconstruction, such as Quantitative Structure Models (QSMs) and circle fitting. Empirical machine learning methods based on predefined point cloud metrics are an alternative. New deep learning approaches offer an even more principled solution, allowing models to learn directly from point clouds. Sparse tensor networks have shown particular promise, transforming forestry tasks such as tree species classification. We investigate whether these strengths can be extended to estimate individual tree DBH from ULS data across four mixed-species temperate forest plots in Germany. We further advance existing sparse tensor networks by introducing TreeNext, a lightweight deep learning architecture for direct individual tree parameter estimation from voxelized LiDAR point clouds. We present a fully automated forest inventory pipeline from individual tree instance segmentation to DBH regression, and quantify the impact of segmentation errors by comparing performance on automatically segmented and ground truth instances. TreeNext achieves an $R^2$ of 0.8355 on automatically segmented trees and 0.9380 on ground truth segments, outperforming strong tabular baselines including Random Forest and XGBoost. Segmentation quality emerges as the dominant error source in the automated pipeline. TreeNext proves highly data-efficient, performing well even in small data scenarios. Our findings establish sparse tensor networks as a practical foundation for automated individual tree DBH estimation from ULS.

</details>


### [2] [GOFish: Full Field-of-View Gaussian Splatting and Mesh Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37818)
*Allen Tu,Meher Gitika Karumuri,Shifan Zhu,Hailing Wang,Yen-Liang Lin,David Held,Matthias Zwicker,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOFish是首个直接在鱼眼图像上训练、可渲染超过180°视场并提取对齐网格的3D高斯泼溅表面重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、自动驾驶和360°相机依赖鱼眼镜头获得大视场，构建仿真就绪的数字孪生需要全视场照片级渲染与对齐的碰撞网格。然而现有鱼眼高斯泼溅方法仅能光栅化至180°且不生成网格，而高斯表面重建方法又假设针孔训练图像，二者无法兼顾。

Method: 提出GOFish：直接以鱼眼图像训练；通过Calibrated Densification在全视场恢复表面；用Calibrated Mip Filtering与Wide-Angle Appearance Modeling将像素模糊与镜头暗角排除在场景与网格之外；从同一场景提取对齐网格。

Result: 在200° FIORD与205° FullCircle基准上，PSNR相较最优先前方法分别提升2.28 dB与1.97 dB；在各自视场内优于鱼眼渲染基线；在FIORD与ScanNet++主集上取得最高网格F-score。

Conclusion: 高斯场景与网格共同构成无需LiDAR、仿真就绪的数字孪生，可实时渲染、光线追踪全视场并提供碰撞几何。

Abstract: Robots, autonomous vehicles, and consumer 360° cameras use fisheye lenses for wide fields of view (FoVs). Simulation-ready digital twins require photorealistic rendering across the full FoV and an aligned collision mesh. However, prior fisheye Gaussian Splatting methods only rasterize up to 180° and do not produce meshes, while Gaussian surface reconstruction methods assume pinhole training images. We present GOFish, the first 3D Gaussian Splatting surface reconstruction method to train directly on fisheye images, rasterize FoVs beyond 180°, and extract an aligned mesh from the same scene. Calibrated densification recovers the surface across the entire FoV, while Calibrated Mip Filtering and Wide-Angle Appearance Modeling keep pixel blur and lens falloff out of the scene and mesh. Across the 200° FIORD and 205° FullCircle benchmarks, GOFish improves PSNR over the best prior method by 2.28 and 1.97 dB, respectively. It also outperforms fisheye rendering baselines within their own FoVs and achieves the highest mesh F-score on FIORD and the main ScanNet++ set. Together, the Gaussian scene and mesh form a LiDAR-free, simulation-ready digital twin that renders in real time, ray traces the full FoV, and provides collision geometry.

</details>


### [3] [Convex Potential Learning for Optimal Transport Between Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37844)
*Hongyu Kan,Ivan Medri,Natasha Ironside,Kristofor Pas,Richard Corp,Shinjini Kundu,Gustavo Kunde Rohde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通过参数化凸势函数来估计最优传输映射的新方法，构造上保证映射无旋（curl-free），与Brenier定理一致；在2D/3D医学影像数据上取得更低的传输估计误差和更高质量的图像重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像间的形态学分析依赖最优传输映射，但现有方法要么难以扩展到大型3D医学图像，要么依赖正则化形式仅得到近似传输映射，精度不足，影响下游医学图像分析效果。

Method: 对凸势函数进行参数化，由其梯度直接构造传输映射，从而在构造上保证映射无旋并满足Brenier定理；在2D与3D医学影像数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验显示该方法较现有方法具有更低的传输估计误差，并产生更高质量的图像重建。

Conclusion: 基于凸势函数参数化的最优传输映射估计可在保证理论一致性的同时提升精度与重建质量，适用于2D/3D医学图像形态学分析。

Abstract: Computing optimal transport maps between medical images plays a fundamental role in morphological analysis. However, existing methods are either difficult to scale to large 3D medical images or rely on regularized formulations that provide only approximate transport maps. To improve the accuracy of downstream medical image analysis, we propose a novel approach for estimating optimal transport maps by parameterizing the convex potential function. By construction, the resulting transport map is guaranteed to be curl-free, consistent with Brenier's theorem. We evaluate the proposed method on both 2D and 3D medical imaging datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves lower transport estimation errors and produces higher-quality image reconstructions than existing methods.

</details>


### [4] [Effects of Deep Learning Architecture and Pretraining on Automated Vocal Fold Segmentation in Ex Vivo High-Speed Phonation Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37860)
*Varun Vinayak Kalaiarasan,Charles Farbos de Luzan,Khalid Malik,Ephraim Gutmark,Liran Oren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次将深度学习用于二维声门内PIV高速图像中的声带自动分割，基准测试了4种架构（UNet、UNet++、TransUNet、Swin-UNet）及ImageNet预训练的影响，发现各模型Dice差异极小，差异主要体现在边界保真度，预训练显著提升Transformer类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维声门内PIV声带离体发声实验图像存在残余眩光、组织-流场对比度低、示踪粒子散斑等问题，导致声带边界模糊，现有方法依赖人工分割，效率低且主观。

Method: 基于16,344张专家标注图像（227次离体犬喉发声试验），训练六条流水线：CNN类UNet与UNet++、混合CNN-Transformer的TransUNet、纯Transformer的Swin-UNet（后两者分别从头训练与ImageNet预训练）。采用5个随机种子、标准化协议训练，在50个留出测试试验上用试验级Wilcoxon符号秩检验（Holm校正）比较DSC、BF1、HD95。

Result: 平均DSC仅0.9734–0.9764，模型差异主要在边界保真度。预训练显著改善TransUNet与Swin-UNet的BF1和HD95，Swin-UNet提升约为TransUNet的4倍；预训练后两者表现相近，但Swin-UNet可训练参数量仅约四分之一。无预训练时，TransUNet的BF1显著更高、HD95更低，HD95>50像素图像占比约为CNN类模型的一半。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维声门内PIV声带分割提供了首个深度学习基准，并为噪声光学条件或高度可变形对象的实验流场可视化图像分割提供参考框架。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) intraglottal particle image velocimetry (PIV) of ex vivo phonation experiments quantifies vocal fold medial surface dynamics and the intraglottal flow field. However, it relies on manual segmentation because residual glare, low tissue-to-flow contrast, and tracer-particle speckle obscure the vocal fold boundaries. This study assessed how deep learning architecture and ImageNet pretraining affect automated vocal fold segmentation in these high-speed images. Four established architectures from three categories were benchmarked: the convolutional neural network (CNN)-based UNet and UNet++, the hybrid CNN-transformer-based TransUNet, and the pure transformer-based Swin-UNet, the latter two trained from scratch and from ImageNet-pretrained weights. Using 16,344 expert-annotated images from 227 phonation trials of excised canine larynges, the six pipelines were trained with five seeds under a standardized protocol and compared on 50 held-out test trials using trial-level Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. Mean Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) spanned only 0.9734 to 0.9764, so the pipelines differed mainly in boundary fidelity. Pretraining significantly improved the boundary F1 score (BF1) and 95th percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95) of the TransUNet and Swin-UNet, about four times more for the Swin-UNet. Once pretrained, the two performed similarly, although the Swin-UNet has only about a quarter of the trainable parameters. Without pretraining, the TransUNet had a significantly higher BF1 and a lower HD95 than both CNN-based pipelines and about half their share of images with HD95 > 50 pixels. To our knowledge, this is the first study to apply deep learning to vocal fold segmentation in 2D intraglottal PIV, and it offers a reference framework for segmenting experimental flow-visualization images with noisy optical conditions or highly deformable objects.

</details>


### [5] [PairCLIP-SWA: Similarity-Guided Balancing and Stochastic Weight Averaging for Generalized Deepfake Image Detection with OpenCLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37874)
*Md. Nakib,Sudipto Sarkar,A. J. A. Jubayer Talukder,Mohammad Ariful Haque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 PairCLIP-SWA：将 OpenCLIP ViT-L/14 视觉塔经渐进式解冻微调，并在权重空间对训练轨迹做平均（SWA），证明权重平均可缓解单检查点选择的不稳定性与验证集误排序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deepfake 检测器通常仅部署在验证集上得分最高的单一检查点，但该选择的可靠性极少被度量；同时验证准确率选点可能误导测试性能。

Method: 单骨干检测器：CLIP 相似度引导的一对一子采样构建类别均衡语料，对 OpenCLIP ViT-L/14 渐进式分块解冻微调，并在权重空间对微调轨迹做滑动平均（SWA）；另做 22 组内部配置消融（含预处理消融）。

Result: DF-Wild-Cup 域内：97.04% 准确率、98.64% AU-ROC、4.07% EER（对比三骨干集成 94.46%/97.60%/5.00%）；未平均检查点在平均区间内验证准确率波动 5.34 点、验证损失翻倍以上，平均权重从不更差且最多优 5.99 点。OpenForensics 派生基准上验证选点 98.43% 但对 10,905 张不相交测试集仅 88.85%（拒真 21.1%），而验证排名最末的终端平均达 94.76%。消融显示 CLIP 预训练而非集成驱动精度，二次人脸裁剪损失约 34 点，频域输入迁移性差。

Conclusion: 权重平均能提升稳定性与泛化，验证准确率选点不可靠；建议采用基于早停划分的逐类选择准则。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors are almost always deployed as the single checkpoint that scored best on a validation split, yet the reliability of that choice is rarely measured. We measure it with PairCLIP-SWA, a single-backbone detector that adapts an OpenCLIP ViT-L/14 visual tower by progressive block unfreezing on a class-balanced corpus built by CLIP-similarity-guided one-to-one subsampling, and averages the fine-tuning trajectory in weight space. Trained in-domain on the IEEE SP Cup 2025 (DF-Wild-Cup) benchmark under the same protocol as the strongest published three-backbone ensemble, the weight-averaged detector reaches 97.04% accuracy, 98.64% AU-ROC and a 4.07% equal error rate, against 94.46%, 97.60% and 5.00%. Its training log shows why averaging matters: over the averaged epochs the non-averaged checkpoint swings across 5.34 points of validation accuracy while its validation loss more than doubles, whereas the average is never worse and is up to 5.99 points better. On an OpenForensics-derived benchmark the same instability becomes a misranking: the checkpoint that validation accuracy selects (98.43%) scores 88.85% on a disjoint 10,905-image test partition, rejecting 21.1% of genuine photographs, while the terminal average, ranked last on validation, scores 94.76%. A study of twenty-two in-house configurations on the identical DF-Wild-Cup split, including preprocessing ablations, shows that CLIP pre-training rather than ensembling drives accuracy, that secondary face cropping costs about 34 points, and that frequency-domain inputs transfer poorly. We recommend per-class selection criteria measured off the early-stopping split.

</details>


### [6] [Intersectional Invariant Learning for Fair Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37881)
*Uzoamaka Ezeakunne,Taiwo Olabamipe,Deevesh Jain,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出交集不变学习（IIL）框架，从特征学习层面而非仅输出调整层面缓解深度伪造检测中的交叉群体公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测模型整体准确率高，但不同人口群体间性能差异显著，部分群体假阳性率明显偏高。现有公平性方法仅通过约束模型输出来均衡群体预测，未解决特征层面的潜在偏差，模型仍可能依赖肤色纹理等人口特征作为间接判别线索。

Method: IIL在"识别最具挑战性的人口划分"与"学习跨这些划分保持一致的特征"之间交替训练；引入假阳性率（FPR）公平性目标，并在六个性别×肤色交叉群体上进行群体均衡采样。

Result: 在含100万+图像、10个基线检测器、21项公平性与效用指标的大规模基准上评测，IIL达到帕累托最优：AUC>99%、FPR<2.5%、交叉公平性差异（FMEO）最低；在21项指标中的绝大多数上优于先前公平性方法，相较最强对比方法将假阳性率与交叉公平性差异降低数倍。

Conclusion: 特征层面的不变学习结合FPR公平目标与群体均衡采样，可有效提升深度伪造检测的交叉群体公平性而不牺牲效用，优于仅调整输出的后处理方法。

Abstract: Deepfake detection models achieve high overall accuracy but exhibit large performance disparities across demographic groups, causing some groups to experience substantially higher false-positive rates than others. Existing fairness-aware methods address this by constraining model outputs to equalize group predictions, but do not tackle the underlying feature-level bias. As a result, models may still rely on demographic characteristics, such as skin texture, as indirect cues for detecting manipulated content. To address this problem, we propose Intersectional Invariant Learning (IIL), a framework that improves feature learning rather than only adjusting model outputs. IIL alternates between identifying the most challenging demographic partitions and learning features that remain consistent across those partitions. The framework also includes a false-positive-rate fairness objective and group-balanced sampling across six gender x skin-tone intersectional groups. We evaluate IIL on a large-scale fairness benchmark containing over one million images, 10 baseline detectors, and 21 fairness and utility metrics. Results show that IIL achieves Pareto-optimal performance, simultaneously attaining an AUC above 99%, a false-positive rate under 2.5%, and the lowest intersectional fairness disparity (FMEO) among all compared methods. IIL outperforms prior fairness-aware methods on the large majority of the 21 evaluation metrics, while reducing both false-positive rates and intersectional fairness disparities by several-fold compared with the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [7] [FYC: FPR-Disparity Weight Perturbation and Youden Calibration for Fair Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37882)
*Uzoamaka Ezeakunne,Taiwo Olabamipe,Deevesh Jain,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段事后方法FYC，通过FPR差异准则下的权重扰动加分组阈值校准，在无需重训与人口统计标注的前提下，在AI-Face v2上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测器整体准确率高，但假阳性率在不同性别与肤色群体间存在两位数差异。现有补救方案要么需要带公平目标的全量重训，要么需要人口统计标注数据，在仅有预训练权重时不可行；而无标签的事后校准器又不改变检测器权重。同时，先前权重扰动工作使用的Demographic Parity目标效果依赖架构、不可预测。

Method: FYC为两阶段事后方法：第一阶段在FPR差异（仅对真实图像条件化的Demographic Parity）准则下对检测器权重进行扰动；第二阶段为每个群体分别校准决策阈值。无需修改架构，也无需全量重训或人口统计标注。

Result: 在AI-Face v2全部已评测检测器上，跨6个性别×肤色交叉群体，FYC在21项公平性与效用指标中的18项上领先，包括专门设计的公平性架构，且训练成本仅为很小一部分。分析表明：原始Demographic Parity目标是否奏效依赖架构且不可预测——4个架构中2个提升、1个基本不变、1个（PG-FDD）显著退化；而将其条件化到真实图像（FPR差异准则）后，4个架构的公平性与准确性均一致提升，无例外。

Conclusion: 以真实图像条件化的FPR差异准则是关键设计，可消除权重扰动方法的架构依赖性，在低成本、免标注、免重训条件下同时改善公平性与效用，为深度伪造检测的事后公平性矫正提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors routinely achieve high aggregate accuracy while exhibiting substantial demographic disparities in their errors: false-positive rates differ by double digits across gender and skin tone. Most existing remedies require either full retraining with a fairness-aware objective or demographic-annotated training data, both impractical when only a pretrained checkpoint is available, while label-free post-hoc calibrators leave the detector's weights unchanged. We introduce FYC (FPR-disparity Weight Perturbation and Youden Calibration), a two-stage post-hoc method that perturbs a detector's weights under an FPR-disparity criterion, then calibrates a separate decision threshold for each demographic group - requiring no architectural changes and no full retraining. Evaluated against every detector benchmarked on AI-Face v2 across six intersectional gender x skin-tone groups, FYC outperforms the field on 18 of 21 reported fairness and utility metrics, including purpose-built fairness architectures, at a fraction of the training cost. We further identify why the choice of fairness criterion is critical: the original Demographic Parity objective used in prior weight-perturbation work is architecture-dependent and unpredictable, improving two of four evaluated architectures, leaving a third largely unchanged, and substantially degrading the fourth (PG-FDD), with no way to anticipate the outcome for a given checkpoint in advance. Conditioning the same objective on real images only - our FPR-disparity criterion - eliminates this unpredictability, improving fairness and accuracy jointly across all four architectures with no exceptions.

</details>


### [8] [Venus-DefakerOne2: A Vision-Forensics Foundation Model with Broader Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37918)
*Chenfan Qu,Yachao Liang,Mingqi Fang,Jiangwei Xie,Zhenming Wang,Baoyue Zhang,Changjiang Jiang,Zheng Chang,Fanli Jin,Xuekang Zhu,Kaiwen Feng,Junchi Li,Weihong Zhang,Haoyue Wang,Chenfeng Zhang,Yinan Xia,Fangming Cui,Dong Fan,Jingjing Liu,Tianyi Zhang,Luobin Chen,Ji-Zhe Zhou,Sheng Li,Wei Wang,Bin Li,Ran He,Xinpeng Zhang,Linna Zhou,Jian Liu,Weiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的防伪造基础模型 Venus-DefakerOne2，将取证能力扩展到数字篡改与物理欺骗、图像与视频的完整威胁谱系，并在多类基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的伪造具有异构性：既包括数字操纵也包括物理欺骗，既存在于图像也包括视频。现有方法虽有进展，但仍缺少覆盖如此广泛威胁谱的统一防伪造基础模型。

Method: 基于数千万开源与自建样本训练，采用两项关键设计：(1) Tide-Style-Tuning，交替使用 LoRA 与全参数微调，在计算效率、泛化性与难样本拟合之间取得平衡，并缓解灾难性遗忘；(2) 基于熵的测试自适应（Entropy-based Test Adaptation），通过分析预测熵确定最优预测模态。

Result: 在综合基准上取得新 SOTA：DeepFake 人脸 96.7%、AI 生成图像 93.3%、篡改文档 87.6%、操纵自然图像 89.6%、物理欺骗 93.1%、AI 生成视频 96.0%，显著优于专用专家模型及前代 DefakerOne。

Conclusion: Venus-DefakerOne2 是一个强大且通用的二分类取证基础模型，沿伪造形式与媒体类型两个维度拓展了取证能力边界。

Abstract: Real-world forgery is inherently heterogeneous: it manifests not only as digital manipulation but also as physical spoofing, and is carried not only by images but also by videos. Despite substantial progress, a unified anti-forgery foundation model covering such a broad threat spectrum remains absent. In this paper, we present Venus-DefakerOne2, which substantially extends the forensic capability boundary along both the forgery-form and media-type dimensions. Trained on tens of millions of open-source and in-house samples, it incorporates two key designs. Tide-Style-Tuning alternates LoRA and full-parameter fine-tuning to balance computational efficiency, generalization, and hard-sample fitting while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Entropy-based Test Adaptation determines the optimal prediction modality by analyzing prediction entropy. Venus-DefakerOne2 achieves new state-of-the-art performance across a comprehensive suite of benchmarks, including DeepFake faces (96.7%), AI-generated images (93.3%), tampered documents (87.6%), manipulated natural images (89.6%), physical spoofs (93.1%), and AI-generated videos (96.0%). It significantly outperforms both specialized expert models and its predecessor, DefakerOne, establishing itself as a powerful and versatile foundation model for binary-decision forensic tasks.

</details>


### [9] [You Don't Need Aligned Representations: Knowledge Distillation via Random Prototype Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37937)
*Hamza Etcibasi,Ramazan Gokberk Cinbis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RPKD 抛弃“师生特征空间需兼容/对齐”的假设，让师生网络均与一个共享的随机初始化、冻结原型库比对，从而在一个外部参考空间中完成对齐；该方法在 CIFAR-100、ImageNet-100、ImageNet-1K 上分别取得最高 +7.98%、+12.44%、+1.28% 的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏领域普遍假设教师与学生的表征必须位于兼容或显式对齐的特征空间中，这限制了跨架构迁移的灵活性；同时，基于 logit 的方法在类别数很少的场景下监督信号会随类别数减少而坍缩。

Method: 构建一个随机初始化并冻结的原型向量库作为共享外部参考空间；教师侧通过无任何可训练参数的固定随机映射投影到该空间，只有学生侧自适应。蒸馏分为两支：logit 分支对齐全局原型相似度分布，特征分支匹配空间分辨的原型激活图；两支均在同一固定原型空间中运作。该空间可视为近似保持样本间相似性结构的随机特征嵌入，因此原型库与蒸馏目标在师生对不同架构间保持不变，仅输入投影按架构实例化。

Result: 在多个数据集上验证有效：相对 OFA，CIFAR-100 最高提升 +7.98%，ImageNet-100 提升 +12.44%，ImageNet-1K 提升 +1.28%。

Conclusion: 将原型词表与任务标签空间解耦后，在低类别场景下可获得比仅用标签空间更丰富的监督信号，这是 logit 类方法所不具备的优势，对标签空间受限的真实应用尤为重要；实验表明 RPKD 在跨架构师生对上具有良好的有效性与可复用性。

Abstract: A widely held assumption in knowledge distillation is that teacher and student representations must occupy a compatible or explicitly aligned feature space. We propose RPKD (Random Prototype Knowledge Distillation), which discards this assumption by comparing both networks against a shared bank of randomly initialized, frozen prototype vectors. Alignment is therefore carried out in an external reference space rather than in the teacher's native feature coordinates: the teacher side is projected by fixed random maps that carry no trainable parameters, and only the student adapts. Two branches handle the transfer: a logit branch aligning global prototype similarity distributions and a feature branch matching spatially-resolved prototype activation maps. Both operate within the same fixed prototype space, which can be interpreted as a random feature embedding that approximately preserves inter-sample similarity structure, so the prototype bank and the distillation objective stay unchanged across teacher-student pairs while only the input projections are instantiated per architecture. In low-category settings, decoupling the prototype vocabulary from the task label space yields richer supervision than the label space alone, an advantage absent from logit-based methods, whose supervisory signal collapses as class count shrinks. This advantage is especially relevant in real-world scenarios where the label space is typically constrained. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of RPKD, achieving a maximum gain of +7.98% over OFA on CIFAR-100, +12.44% on ImageNet-100, and +1.28% on ImageNet-1K. Source code is available at https://github.com/HamzaEtcibasi/RPKD

</details>


### [10] [Meta-Initialization of Implicit Neural Representations to Accelerate Deformable Medical Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37965)
*Marat O. Zinovev,Dmitry V. Sorokin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究用元初始化加速INR形变配准的早期适应，比较Reptile与STRAINER两种方案，二者早期均优于随机初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR配准方法需针对每个新图像对重新优化网络参数，适应速度慢是实际应用瓶颈，作者希望通过历史任务学习到的初始化来加速早期适应。

Method: 以KAN-IDIR为基座INR位移场模型，考察两种初始化方案：Reptile元初始化，以及STRAINER对坐标网络共享成分的迁移。实验在OASIS-1上进行，364个连续图像对用于训练，49对用于测试时适应；以mean Dice、HD95及第1500次迭代时负雅可比行列式体素比例作为评价指标。

Result: 在所研究配置下，两种方案在早期迭代均比随机初始化获得更高Dice和更低HD95；小迭代预算下Reptile数值上优于STRAINER，但二者预训练预算并不匹配。随迭代增加，方案间差异缩小。STRAINER预训练耗时39–40分钟，Reptile为44.98小时。

Conclusion: 从历史配准任务学习初始化可加速INR配准的早期适应，Reptile在早期精度上更优但预训练代价高得多；需在匹配预算下进一步比较以得出可靠结论。

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) methods for deformable medical image registration require the network parameters to be optimized for each new image pair, making adaptation speed a practical limitation. This work investigates whether early adaptation can be accelerated using an initialization learned from a collection of previously solved registration tasks. We examine two schemes: Reptile meta-initialization and STRAINER transfer of the shared component of a coordinate network. KAN-IDIR is used as the base INR displacement-field model. Experiments are conducted on OASIS-1, with 364 consecutive pairs used for training and 49 pairs for test-time adaptation. Registration quality is evaluated using mean Dice and HD95, together with the fraction of voxels with a negative Jacobian determinant at iteration 1500. In the configurations studied, both schemes yield higher Dice and lower HD95 at early iterations than random initialization. Reptile numerically outperforms STRAINER under a small iteration budget, although their pretraining budgets are not matched. As the number of iterations increases, the differences between the schemes shrink. STRAINER pretraining takes 39-40 minutes, compared with 44.98 hours for Reptile.

</details>


### [11] [Hierarchical Diffusion for Camera-controlled Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37983)
*Kaihua Chen,Yiming Gong,Deva Ramanan,Tarasha Khurana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WanAR+FixAR 轻量框架：将视频生成重构为 warp+inpaint 问题，并用分层长上下文去噪（先短时条件生成、再长程稀疏历史细化）实现单图可控相机的长视频生成，缓解误差累积与相机控制歧义，在 RealEstate10k 与 DL3DV 上优于此前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归长视频生成存在两大问题：(1) 误差累积/漂移——远端帧条件于已生成输出而非真值；(2) 相机控制融合方式不明确。现有方案如 self-forcing 与显式 6-DOF 位姿条件，或计算开销大，或随时间产生场景几何漂移。

Method: 采用 WanAR（自回归生成）+ FixAR（分层长上下文去噪）的组合。借鉴前馈新视角合成思路，把视频生成改写为 warping-and-inpainting 任务，以约束场景几何与输入相机一致；去噪分阶段、各阶段使用不同时间历史：先基于近邻历史生成帧，再由第二个去噪器基于时间下采样的长程历史进行细化，实现由细到粗（fine-to-coarse）的全局一致性，避免全程长上下文的高昂代价。

Result: 在 RealEstate10k 与 DL3DV 数据集上，WanAR+FixAR 优于既有相机控制长视频生成方法，输出在时间稳定性与输入相机轨迹一致性两方面均表现更佳。

Conclusion: 通过将相机控制转化为 warp-inpaint 表述并引入分层长上下文去噪，可在低成本下让每帧有效利用完整时间上下文，同时保持几何一致与时间稳定，为单图驱动的可控长视频生成提供简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: We address the task of long-video, camera-controlled generation from a single image. The dominant autoregressive paradigm suffers from two key limitations: (1) error accumulation over time, as distant frames are conditioned on previously generated outputs rather than ground truth, leading to drift; and (2) ambiguity in how to incorporate camera control. Standard solutions to both--such as self-forcing and conditioning on explicit 6-DOF poses--either incur significant computational overhead or produce drifting scene geometries over time. We propose a simple and lightweight framework that combines autoregressive generation (WanAR) with hierarchical long-context denoising (FixAR). To enforce scene geometry remains consistent with input cameras, we draw from feed-forward novel-view synthesis and reformulate video generation as a warping-and-inpainting problem. To mitigate drift, we introduce a hierarchical denoising strategy in which different stages condition on different temporal histories. Specifically, we first generate frames conditioned on the immediate past, and then refine them using a second denoiser conditioned on a temporally subsampled long-range history to enforce global consistency. This fine-to-coarse scheme enables each generated frame to effectively condition on the full temporal context without incurring a prohibitive cost. Experiments on RealEstate10k and DL3DV demonstrate that WanAR+FixAR outperforms prior methods for camera-controlled long-video generation, producing results that are both temporally stable and consistent with input camera trajectories.

</details>


### [12] [Frequency-Adaptive Gaussian Grids for Efficient Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38007)
*Anatolii Evdokimov,Stavros Diolatzis,Bingxuan Li,Akshay Jindal,Patsorn Sangkloy,Qi Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gaussian-Grid 提出一种混合神经表示，按局部频率内容在 2D 高斯与其附着的多分辨率特征网格之间动态分配计算，从而自适应整合结构化与非结构化基元；在高分辨率自然图像上实现更优的重建保真度与内存占用，并在 GPU 原生数据集存储场景中较 JPEG+ZIP 取得 1.24–2.14× 解码加速和 23–176× 的 PCIe 流量削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表示可缓解对更高分辨率图像的需求，但结构化表示（特征网格，具空间一致性）与非结构化表示（2D 高斯，可自适应捕捉局部方差）之间存在根本性权衡，选择单一表示会显著影响图像的表示质量，因此需要一种能兼顾二者优势的自适应方案。

Method: 提出 Gaussian-Grid：一种混合神经表示，将 2D 高斯分布与其附着的多分辨率特征网格结合；依据局部频率内容在高斯与网格之间动态划分计算量，使架构自动匹配目标信号的固有复杂度；在高分辨率自然图像上进行评估，并应用于 GPU 原生数据集存储。

Result: 在高分辨率自然图像上，相比 JPEG+ZIP 数据归档，解码速度提升 1.24–2.14×，PCIe 数据传输量降低 23–176×；在多种数据集上保持较小内存占用的同时实现高重建保真度。

Conclusion: Gaussian-Grid 通过自适应融合结构化与非结构化基元，有效缓解了两类神经表示之间的固有权衡，可在低内存开销下实现高保真重建，并具备作为 GPU 原生数据集存储方案的实用价值。

Abstract: Neural representation has emerged as a powerful tool to mitigate the demand for increasingly higher-resolution images. However, a fundamental trade-off remains between structured (e.g., feature grids, which offer spatial consistency) and unstructured (e.g., 2D Gaussians, which adaptively capture local variances) representations. This choice significantly affects how well an algorithm represents an image. Here, we present Gaussian-Grid, a hybrid neural representation that adaptively integrates structured and unstructured primitives. By dynamically partitioning computation between 2D Gaussian distributions and their attached multi-resolution feature grids based on the local frequency content, Gaussian-Grid automatically optimizes its architecture to match the underlying complexity of the target signal. We evaluate our approach using high-resolution natural images and demonstrate its applications as GPU-native dataset storage, showing $1.24$-$2.14\times$ decoding speed improvement and PCIe data traffic reduction of $23\times$-$176\times$ over JPEG + ZIP data archiving. The results show that Gaussian-Grid maintains small memory footprints while achieving high reconstruction fidelity across a variety of datasets.

</details>


### [13] [Where Can We Fool the Model's Mind? Response-Guided Semantic Probing and Adaptive Routing for Transferable Attacks on Black-Box MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38013)
*Ahmed Abdelnaby,Mohamed Elmahallawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISTA黑盒对抗攻击框架，通过从MLLM响应中构建不确定性语义脆弱性图并引导扰动分配，在GPT-5、Gemini-3.5-Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.6上实现最高97%攻击成功率且PSNR>30dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对多模态大模型的迁移黑盒攻击依赖替代模型的显著性来分配扰动，而这种显著性未必反映未知目标MLLM在语义上如何解读或倚重不同图像区域，导致迁移效果受限。作者希望在不获取梯度、logits、参数或内部表示的前提下，从模型响应层面识别语义脆弱区域。

Method: VISTA（Vulnerability-Informed Semantic Transfer Attacks）框架，仅依赖MLLM响应：1) 构建基于不确定性的多模态行为（UBMB）图，从响应漂移、冲突、误导与语义偏移中刻画区域脆弱性；2) 采用熵自适应Sinkhorn路由进行扰动分配；3) 结合替代模型集成聚焦脆弱区域。支持两种设定：VISTA-Proxy（纯迁移，探测开源MLLM后迁移至未查询的商业模型）与VISTA-Victim（查询辅助，直接探测目标模型捕获其专属语义脆弱性）。

Result: 在GPT-5、Gemini-3.5-Flash与Claude Sonnet 4.6上，攻击成功率最高达97%，同时PSNR保持在30 dB以上；VISTA-Proxy在不查询最终受害模型的情况下最高达93% ASR。

Conclusion: 由响应推导出的语义脆弱性可有效支撑跨异构MLLM的对抗迁移，说明语义层面的脆弱区域识别比传统替代模型显著性更具迁移性，为无梯度黑盒攻击提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) ground language in visual evidence, yet their semantic reasoning can be exploited through transferable black-box adversarial attacks. Existing attacks typically allocate perturbations using surrogate-model saliency, which may not reflect how an unseen MLLM semantically interprets or relies on different image regions. We introduce VISTA (Vulnerability-Informed Semantic Transfer Attacks), a black-box framework that identifies semantically vulnerable visual regions from an MLLM's responses without access to gradients, logits, parameters, or internal representations. VISTA supports two attack settings: (i) VISTA-Proxy, a pure-transfer setting that probes an open-source MLLM and transfers the resulting adversarial examples to unseen commercial MLLMs without querying them; and (ii) VISTA-Victim, a query-assisted setting that probes the target MLLM to capture model-specific semantic vulnerabilities. VISTA builds an Uncertainty-Based Multimodal Behavior (UBMB) map from response drift, conflict, misleading, and semantic shifts, then uses entropy-adaptive Sinkhorn routing and a surrogate ensemble to focus perturbations on vulnerable regions. Across GPT-5, Gemini-3.5-Flash, and Claude Sonnet 4.6, VISTA achieves up to 97 % attack success rate (ASR) while maintaining a Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) above 30 dB. Notably, VISTA-Proxy achieves up to 93 % ASR without querying the final victim, demonstrating that response-derived semantic vulnerability can enable effective transfer across heterogeneous MLLMs.

</details>


### [14] [Can Event Detectors Leave the Broadcast Camera? Evidence from Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38042)
*David Soeiro-Vuong,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发布一个含345条帧级弹跳/击球标注的网球事件基准（8段多视角视频），用混淆感知匹配器、bootstrap区间与盲式二次标注评测：广播视角监督法近乎完美但手机/场边视角失效；免训练仲裁层RallyArb在手机/场边保持弹跳F1>0.8但击球时序不准；视觉语言模型可跨视角但约0.1 s偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网球统计源自弹跳与击球事件，而单目视频中二者易混淆；现有评测多集中于广播视角，缺乏跨视角（广播、固定高机位、手机、场边）且区分弹跳/击球的帧级基准，导致方法泛化性不明。

Method: 构建含8段视频、345条帧级标注的基准，固定开发/测试划分，设计混淆感知匹配器、片段级bootstrap置信区间，并对3段做盲式二次标注。评测三类方法：带已发表广播权重的监督精确检测器、两个零样本视觉语言模型、以及RallyArb——在球轨迹与球员姿态上做仲裁、不使用事件标签拟合任何参数。

Result: 监督检测器在广播素材近乎完美，在手机与场边素材几乎失效，E2E-Spot池化弹跳F1仅0.130。RallyArb在三段手机/场边片段上弹跳F1>0.8（其中两段用于调参），在留出片段上11个弹跳命中8个，但击球位置偏离接触帧过大，不匹配人类时序。视觉语言模型在各视角均能检出事件，接触帧误差约0.1 s。以其广播权重做留一片段微调后，检测器在两段手机/场边片段上弹跳F1约0.8，但在擦网手机角度仍仅0.2。

Conclusion: 跨视角泛化是网球事件检测的关键瓶颈：广播权重迁移性差，而基于轨迹与姿态的免训练仲裁在困难视角更稳健但时序精度不足。作者公开标注、评测器及所有评测行的冻结预测，以支持可复现比较。

Abstract: Ball bounces and racket hits are the events from which tennis statistics are derived, and on monocular video the two are easy to confuse. We release a benchmark of 345 bounce and hit annotations at frame level on eight clips that mix broadcast, fixed elevated, phone and court-level viewpoints, with a frozen development and test split, a confusion-aware matcher, clip-level bootstrap intervals and a blind second annotation of three clips. On it we score supervised precise spotters with published broadcast weights, two zero-shot vision-language models, and RallyArb, an arbitration layer over a ball track and player poses that fits no parameter on event labels. The supervised spotters score near-perfectly on broadcast footage and almost nothing on phone and court-level footage, where the pooled bounce F1 of E2E-Spot is 0.130. RallyArb keeps a bounce F1 above 0.8 on the three phone and court-level clips, two of which were used to tune it, and finds 8 of 11 bounces on the held-out one, but it places hits too far from the contact frame to match human timing. The vision-language models detect events on every viewpoint and miss the contact frame by about 0.1 s. Fine-tuned leave-one-clip-out from its broadcast weights, the spotter reaches about 0.8 bounce F1 on two phone and court-level clips and stays at 0.2 on a grazing phone angle. The annotations, the evaluator and the frozen predictions of every scored row are released.

</details>


### [15] [Classifying Urban Locations by Cyclist Facility Usage and Directional Alignment: A Validated Camera-Based Framework from Multi-Site Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38045)
*Ao He,Mohit Gupta,Deborah Salon,Ricardo Eiris,Vivian W. H. Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究利用多点位运动触发相机（19次部署、17个地点）与全量人工校验的自动化流程，刻画骑行者对自行车道/人行道的使用分配及逆行行为，发现车道占比越高逆行率越低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管理机构投资自行车设施，却缺乏评估骑行者是否按设计使用这些设施的工具；同时需要检验设施配置是否影响使用模式，以及更高的自行车道使用率是否与更少的逆行相关。

Method: 2023年在亚利桑那州Tempe的17个地点开展19次运动触发相机部署；人工复核识别1963次骑行事件，剔除20次过街后保留1943条标注事件；对同一影像运行自动化流水线，计数达人工总数的92.9%（分部署r=0.99），所有自动逆行判定均经目视验证；按设施使用份额阈值对部署进行分类（自行车道主导9、人行道主导6、混合4）；采用Spearman相关分析车道份额与逆行率关系，并用同城志愿者独立计数做方向模式交叉验证。

Result: 自行车道内骑行者逆行比例为4.6%，人行道上骑行者相对近侧车流方向逆行为49.6%；跨部署层面，自行车道份额与逆行率呈显著负相关（Spearman rho=-0.87，p<0.001，n=16）；同城志愿者计数显示类似的人行道方向模式（rho=0.79）。

Conclusion: 研究将多地点静态相机观测与全人工验证基准相结合，可用于刻画设施使用与行进方向；该工作流为规划者提供低成本筛查手段，识别人行道骑行比例高、相对近侧车流逆行的地点，支持后续详细现场调查。

Abstract: Objectives: Agencies invest in bicycle facilities but lack tools to assess whether cyclists use them as designed. We examine whether usage patterns follow facility configurations and whether higher bike lane usage is associated with less wrong-way riding.
  Methods: Motion-triggered cameras covered 19 deployments at 17 locations in Tempe, Arizona, in 2023. Manual review identified 1,963 cycling events; excluding 20 crossings left 1,943 labeled events for analysis. An automated pipeline on the same captures counted 92.9 percent of the full manual total (per-deployment r = 0.99), and every automated wrong-way call was visually verified. Deployments were classified using thresholds on facility usage shares.
  Findings: Deployments were bike-lane dominant (9), sidewalk dominant (6), or mixed (4). Cyclists in bike lanes rode against the flow in 4.6 percent of events; on sidewalks, riders opposed the near-side traffic flow in 49.6 percent. Across deployments, bike lane share and wrong-way rate were negatively correlated (Spearman rho = -0.87, p < 0.001, n = 16). An independent volunteer count in the same city showed a similar sidewalk-direction pattern (rho = 0.79).
  Novelty: The study combines multi-site still-camera observations with a fully hand-verified benchmark to characterize facility use and travel direction.
  Practical Applications: The workflow offers planners a low-cost screen for locations with high sidewalk cycling and travel against the near-side traffic flow, supporting more detailed site investigation.

</details>


### [16] [Vision Tomography: Towards Portable 3D Bidirectional Face-Skull Inference from Visual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38065)
*Zidu Wang,Meng Xu,Miao Xu,Hengyuan Ma,Jiankuo Zhao,Xutao Li,Zhen Lei,Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"视觉断层成像"(Vision Tomography, VT)框架，可从图像、素描等多种视觉模态重建配对的三维人脸-颅骨几何，并支持人脸与颅骨的联合编辑；核心是可双向推断的人脸-颅骨形变模型BFSM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CT虽能同时获取配对的3D人脸与颅骨几何，但成本高、设备固定、且带来辐射暴露；远程诊断、数字医疗、法医鉴定、失踪人员调查与考古分析等场景急需便携、安全、可及的三维颅骨采集方案。

Method: 1) 构建含200余例样本的数据集，每例包含CT颅骨、CT人脸及高保真带纹理人脸扫描；2) 提出密集射线匹配(dense ray matching)配准方法，保证人脸、颅骨及其软组织对应关系的拓扑一致性；3) 构建三维双向人脸-颅骨形变模型(BFSM)，通过共享系数空间实现人脸与颅骨间的形状互推，并对软组织厚度变化建模，从而支持同一颅骨到多张人脸的"一对多"重建（如年龄、体脂变化）。

Result: 在图像或素描重建配对3D人脸-颅骨、以及通过人脸-颅骨联合编辑预测手术效果等法医与医疗应用中展示了有效性；大量实验验证了方法的鲁棒性与准确性。

Conclusion: VT提供了一种低成本、非接触、可便携的CT替代思路，实现了跨模态的配对人脸-颅骨重建与联合操控；代码已开源。

Abstract: Traditional computed tomography (CT) can capture paired 3D face and skull geometry, but it remains costly, fixed in location, and exposes patients to radiation. For applications such as remote diagnostics, digital healthcare, forensic identification, missing-person investigation, and archaeological analysis, there is an urgent need for a portable, safe, and accessible solution to capture 3D skull geometry. In this work, we introduce Vision Tomography (VT), a concept and framework capable of reconstructing paired 3D face-skull geometry from various visual modalities, and also enabling joint manipulation of 3D facial and skull geometry. To achieve this, we first construct a dataset of over 200 samples, where each case includes a CT-based skull, a CT-based face, and a high-fidelity textured face scan. Secondly, we propose a novel dense ray matching registration method that ensures topological consistency across face, skull, and their tissue correspondences. Building on this registration, we introduce the 3D Bidirectional Face-Skull Morphable Model (BFSM) as the foundation of Vision Tomography, which enables shape inference between the face and skull through a shared coefficient space, while also modeling tissue thickness variation to support one-to-many facial reconstructions from the same skull, reflecting individual facial changes such as age or body fat. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of Vision Tomography across various forensic and medical applications, including paired 3D face-skull reconstruction from images or sketches and predicting surgical outcomes with joint face-skull editing. Extensive experiments confirm the robustness and accuracy of our method. Vision Tomography is available at https://github.com/wang-zidu/BFSM

</details>


### [17] [From Next Day Wildfire to Next Days Wildfire: A New Multi-Continent Dataset of Active-Fire Spread for Sequence-to-Sequence Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38086)
*Jose Guillen,Renzo Zanca,Valentin Barriere*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出多模态野火蔓延数据集AFSeq与自回归模型UTAE-AR。AFSeq涵盖2023–2026年8国1,194场火灾，侧重现有基准缺失的短时小火；UTAE-AR在UTAE编码器后、解码器前加入循环模块，实现整段序列的自回归逐日预测。以约21.5%的参数量在WSTS、WSTS+和AFSeq上取得次日精度SOTA，并能长时程稳定预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如WSTS）存在两大局限：数据规模使其仅能覆盖燃烧数周的大火，且火灾来自相似环境，导致样本偏倚、泛化性受限。同时现有模型多为单步预测，无法输出逐日滚动预报。

Method: 1）构建AFSeq：1,194个近期（2023–2026）火灾事件，来自3个区域8个国家，以短时小火为主；2）提出UTAE-AR：在经典U-Net+时序自编码器（UTAE）架构上，于解码器前引入循环模块，建模时序依赖；3）采用自回归推理，将上一日预测的火灾掩膜回馈为输入，逐日外推整段序列。

Result: 在WSTS、WSTS+与AFSeq三个数据集上，次日预测精度均优于现有SOTA，参数仅为对比方法的21.5%；且具备长时程可靠预测能力，单步模型无法做到这一点。

Conclusion: AFSeq弥补了现有基准在火灾规模与环境多样性上的不足；UTAE-AR以更少参数、更强长时程能力实现自回归逐日蔓延预报，验证了轻量循环结构与自回归推理在野火时序预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Benchmarks such as WildfireSpreadTS (WSTS) have enabled data-driven forecasting of wildfire spread from multimodal satellite time series, but they suffer from limitations: size restricts them to large fires that burn for weeks, and they are from similar environments. We introduce AFSeq, a multimodal dataset of 1,194 recent fire events (2023 to 2026) from 8 different countries across three regions, dominated by the short, small fires that existing benchmarks underrepresent. Our dataset is the largest and most diverse to date for time-series-based wildfire spread prediction. We also introduce UTAE-AR, an extension of a classical U-Net with Temporal Auto Encoder, enhanced with recurrent modules before the decoder. This allows the model to not only predict the next day but the whole sequence in an autoregressive way. Moreover, our model can feed its own predicted fire mask back one day at a time, producing the day-by-day forecasts that single-step models cannot. Our results show that UTAE-AR surpasses state-of-the-art on both WSTS, WSTS+ and AFSeq for next-day accuracy while having 21.5% of their parameters, and being able to predict confidently at a longer horizon.

</details>


### [18] [The Prestige: Benchmarking Cognitive Visual Reasoning using Magic Tricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38103)
*Shuo Wen,Beixi Du,Edwin Meriaux,Junming Shi,Chloe Si,David Meger,Doina Precup,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 The Prestige 视频理解基准，利用专业魔术表演中的误导机制考察视觉语言模型的认知视觉推理能力，发现最强模型与人类仍有显著差距，并揭示评分与问题格式层面的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准多停留在表层识别与描述，难以检验模型是否真正追踪事件、进行因果推理。魔术表演中刻意设计的误导（misdirection）天然要求观察者抵抗干扰、回溯事件链并解释反直觉结果，因此可作为认知视觉推理的高难度探针。

Method: 构建 The Prestige 基准，基于专业魔术视频设计开放式问题，覆盖时序理解、因果理解以及对对抗性问题的鲁棒性等维度；对主流开源与闭源 VLM 进行评测并与人类表现对比；分析人工评分与自动评分的一致性差异、归纳模型的反复性失败模式，并做消融实验，比较开放生成与多项选择两种问题格式下的表现。

Result: 评测显示，即便最强模型与人类表现之间仍存在显著差距；人工评分与自动评分之间存在系统性偏差；模型呈现若干反复出现的失败模式；消融实验表明，模型在无法独立生成正确解释的情况下，仍能在多项选择中选出正确答案。

Conclusion: 研究结果同时暴露了 VLM 推理能力的不足与其评估方式的缺陷，说明需要更可靠的视频理解模型以及更严谨的基准设计与评分方法，并提示问题格式本身会显著影响评测结论。

Abstract: We introduce The Prestige, a video understanding benchmark that probes cognitive visual reasoning through professional magic performances, where deliberate misdirection challenges models to track events and explain unexpected outcomes. The benchmark comprises open-ended questions ranging from temporal and causal understanding to robustness against adversarial questions. Evaluation of leading open-source and proprietary VLMs reveals a substantial gap between even the strongest model and human performance. We also find systematic discrepancies between human and automated grading and identify recurring model failure modes. Ablations further show that models can select correct multiple-choice answers even when they fail to generate correct explanations independently, motivating further investigations into question formats. Together, these findings highlight limitations in both VLM reasoning and its evaluation, underscoring the need for more reliable video understanding models and more rigorous benchmarks.

</details>


### [19] [What Does LOL Measure? Hidden Label Shifts in Low-Light Enhancement Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38121)
*Adityan Thirumani Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文审计LOL-v1与LOLv2-Real两个低光照增强基准，发现其测试标签与训练标签分布不一致（渲染方式不同、亮度偏移、测试集与训练集重叠），并且已发表方法的测试时调参实质上在补偿这些偏移，导致基准排名不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LOL-v1与LOLv2-Real是监督式低光照图像增强的标准基准，多个代表性方法在评测脚本中附带手工设定的测试时调整。作者怀疑这些基准本身存在标签分布偏移，而现有评测流程未加区分地把"可在训练数据上标定的增益"与"只能在测试集上调出的增益"混为一谈，从而使排行榜失真。

Method: 用统一的指标实现复现8个方法共18个公开检查点的已发表数值（误差在0.09 dB以内），据此审计两个基准：(1)比对LOL-v1测试标签与LOLv2同源采集图像的渲染差异；(2)统计LOLv2-Real测试块（第690–789幅）与训练集标签的亮度分布差异（Cliff's delta、p值），并检查输入统计；(3)核查LOL-v1测试输入在LOLv2-Real中的重现情况；(4)把增益分解为可在训练集标定的部分与仅在测试集上调出的残差，在LOLv2-Syn（无偏移）上作对照。

Result: (i) LOL-v1的15张测试标签与LOLv2同源图像标签亮度相同但饱和度低1.48倍，而LOL-v1自身训练标签与LOLv2渲染一致，已在官方发布版上确认；(ii) LOLv2-Real测试块标签比训练标签暗14%（Cliff's delta=-0.45, p<1e-12），所考察的输入统计无相应偏移，无监督训练的方法在测试集上比训练集高至多5.5 dB；(iii) LOL-v1的15张测试输入全部出现在LOLv2-Real中，其中12张位于其训练集。测试时残差中位数在无偏移的LOLv2-Syn上为0.00 dB，LOL-v1为0.37 dB，LOLv2-Real为0.92 dB（最高3.9 dB）。在偏移下，测试集检查点选择使从零训练模型虚高0.9–2.0 dB，移除已发布的测试时设置会改变第一名的方法。

Conclusion: 两个主流基准的测试标签与训练标签不一致，已发表的测试时调参在很大程度上是在补偿基准缺陷而非提升真实泛化；基于这些基准的排名与增益报告具有误导性。作者提出具体的报告规范建议，以区分训练集可标定的调参与测试集调参。

Abstract: LOL-v1 and LOLv2-Real are the standard benchmarks for supervised low-light image enhancement, and several leading methods ship hand-set test-time adjustments with their evaluation scripts. We audit both benchmarks with 18 public checkpoints of eight methods under one metric implementation that reproduces every published number to within 0.09 dB, and find that their test labels differ from their training labels. (i) The 15 LOL-v1 test labels are rendered differently: the same captures appear in LOLv2 with labels of identical luminance but 1.48x lower saturation, and LOL-v1's own training labels match the LOLv2 rendering; we confirm this on the official release. (ii) The LOLv2-Real test set is the contiguous block of images 690-789, whose labels are 14% darker than the training labels (Cliff's delta = -0.45, p < 1e-12) with no comparable shift in the input statistics we examined; methods trained without any labels score up to 5.5 dB higher on test than on train. (iii) All 15 LOL-v1 test inputs reappear in LOLv2-Real, 12 in its training set. The released test-time settings are consistent with compensating for these shifts. Separating what can be calibrated on training data from what only test-set tuning finds, the test-only residual has median 0.00 dB on LOLv2-Syn, which has no shift, but 0.37 dB on LOL-v1 and 0.92 dB on LOLv2-Real (up to 3.9 dB). Under the shift, test-set checkpoint selection inflates a from-scratch model by 0.9-2.0 dB, and removing the released settings changes which method ranks first. We give concrete reporting recommendations.

</details>


### [20] [StepControl: Long-Horizon Human Motion Generation from Foot-Contact Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38153)
*Hojun Jang,Jinseok Bae,Donggeun Lim,Taesun Kim,Seungbin Jeong,Younghwan Lee,Yong Seok Kim,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StepControl，一种以未来左右脚触地时间表为唯一条件的自回归扩散模型，实现全身动作的可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控人体动作生成主要关注"做什么动作"或"身体移到哪里"，缺少仅通过时间维度（触地节律）控制全身动作的互补性研究。

Method: 将脚部接触表示为显式时间事件，通过专用 cross-attention 注入条件，并结合 classifier-free guidance；引入随机混合噪声调度与 warmup 策略以稳定长时程自回归生成。

Result: 触地时序跟随精度优于将现有空间引导生成器改造为接触约束的方案；在长连续 rollout 中保持有效控制，并能响应节奏变化、走走停停、跛行步态与双脚跳跃等编辑后的时间表。

Conclusion: 极简的脚部触地时间表即可作为有效且直观的时间接口，实现可控动作生成。

Abstract: Controllable human motion generation has largely focused on specifying what action should occur or where the body should move. We study a complementary question: can full-body motion be controlled by specifying only when each foot contacts the ground? We introduce StepControl, an autoregressive diffusion model conditioned on a future binary left/right foot-contact schedule, without requiring target root and body poses, or semantic prompts. StepControl represents contact as explicit temporal events and injects them through dedicated cross-attention with classifier-free guidance. We further introduce a stochastic mixture noise schedule and warmup strategy for stable long-horizon autoregressive generation. Experiments show that StepControl follows prescribed contact timing more accurately than adapting existing spatially guided motion generators to contact constraints. It maintains effective contact control over long continuous rollouts and responds to edited schedules such as cadence changes, stop-and-go pauses, limping gaits, and bilateral jumping. Our results show that minimal foot-contact schedules provide an effective and intuitive temporal interface for controllable motion generation.

</details>


### [21] [REEL: Same Frames, Different Answers and Different Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38158)
*Xin Liang,Jiangpeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 REEL 评测框架，在完全相同的观测帧上比较三种"记忆"表示（Text 文本摘要、Visual 编码器输出、Prefix State 语言模型前缀），以配对正确率变化、累计 GPU 时间和存储大小三项指标评测视频问答系统，揭示"复用计算"与"丢弃信息"两类效率优化在指标上的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答中"看一次、建记忆、后问答"已成主流范式，但记忆内容在问题提出之前就已确定，被丢弃的信息无法事后恢复；同时现有评估只看 token 数，而 token 数只能反映输入变短，无法反映模型复用已计算状态所带来的节省。因此需要一个能把"表示选择"这一变量的影响单独隔离出来的评测方法。

Method: REEL 固定输入观测帧，仅改变保存的表示形式，使回答变化只能归因于记忆内容：Text 保存事件短文记录；Visual 保存编码器输出；Prefix State 额外保存语言模型前缀状态；统一以全量重处理（Frames）为参照。评测维度为配对正确率变化、累计 GPU 时间与存储大小，覆盖 6 个视觉语言模型、2 个基准。

Result: Text 与 Frames 在 20%–29% 的问题上正确性不同，即便平均准确率几乎持平，说明均值掩盖了系统性差异；Visual 与 Prefix State 则显著更接近 Frames。在存储不受限条件下，Prefix State 是"几乎保留 Frames 全部正确回答"的记忆中累计 GPU 时间最低者，且在两个基准上对所有模型均成立；代价是存储，单视频最高约 21 GiB，而 Text 仅需 KB 级。

Conclusion: 复用计算与丢弃信息表面上都表现为"效率"，但只有后者移除了后续问题可能需要的证据，而平均准确率与 token 计数都无法区分二者。REEL 使这种区分对任何"以单一记忆回答多问题"的系统可测量，提示评测应从 token 数转向以配对正确性与真实计算成本为核心的度量。

Abstract: Video understanding keeps moving toward longer recordings, and the longer a recording is, the more questions can be asked about it. But processing it again for every question does not scale, so many systems watch the video once, build a memory, and answer later questions from it. Yet what to remember is decided before anyone knows what will be asked, and whatever is left out cannot be recovered later.
  Moreover, whether the savings pay off is judged by token counts, which fall when the input gets shorter but not when the model reuses what it has already computed. We present REEL, an evaluation framework that compares three saved representations built from exactly the same observed frames, so that a change in answers can come only from what the system remembered. Text writes a short record of what happened, Visual keeps the encoder outputs, and Prefix State also keeps the language-model prefix. Each is compared with full reprocessing (Frames) by paired correctness changes, cumulative GPU time, and storage size. Across six vision-language models and two benchmarks, Text and Frames differ in correctness on 20\% to 29\% of questions, even when their mean accuracy is almost the same. By contrast, Visual and Prefix State stay much closer to Frames. With unlimited storage, Prefix State is the cheapest in total GPU time among memories that keep almost all of the answers Frames gets right, for every model on both benchmarks. The trade-off is storage: Prefix State keeps up to about 21 GiB per video, whereas Text needs only kilobytes. Reusing computation and discarding information both look like efficiency, but only the second removes evidence that later questions may need, and neither mean accuracy nor token count tells them apart. REEL makes that difference measurable for any system that answers many questions from one saved memory.

</details>


### [22] [Neighbourhood shortcuts in building damage assessment from very-high-resolution satellite imagery: diagnosis and a building-evidence plug-in](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38162)
*JianChong Guo,YuTing Wan,Kefan Li,AiLong Ma,YanFei Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出 BDA 模型的高总体精度掩盖了对"邻域捷径"的依赖，并通过邻域置换等诊断实验量化该问题，进而提出无需重训的 building-evidence 插件加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害损毁具有空间聚集性，多数建筑与邻居损毁等级一致，总体精度被这类样本主导；而与周围不一致的孤立损毁建筑稀少、易被忽视。因此高总分无法判别模型是真在评估建筑自身，还是借邻居做捷径。

Method: 按建筑与邻居的一致性分层；在保持像素不变的前提下把建筑迁入新邻域；覆盖 BDA 主要范式的九个模型进行诊断。提出 building-evidence 插件：为现有模型加两个专家且不重训——一个仅看建筑本身，一个看周边但用无缝置换的邻域训练以学会不信任邻域。

Result: 所有模型漏检 1/3 至 1/2 的孤立损毁建筑；非洪涝事件中，完好建筑迁入损毁邻域后有 73–87% 被判为损毁。插件在九个模型上保持或提升总体精度，与邻居不一致建筑的误差下降 8–18%，被邻域"带偏"的完好建筑减少 40–69%，合成孤立损毁检测率由 1–6% 升至 13–39%，并可迁移到光学–SAR 数据与未见地震场景。

Conclusion: 高总体分数确实掩盖了对邻域的系统性依赖；引入建筑级证据可低成本削弱这一捷径，提升模型对建筑自身的判据可靠性。

Abstract: Building damage assessment (BDA) from very-high-resolution imagery supports disaster response, and deep models now achieve high overall accuracy on public benchmarks. Because damage is spatially clustered, most buildings share the damage level of their neighbours, so overall scores are dominated by such buildings, whereas damaged buildings among intact ones are rare and easily overlooked. A high overall score therefore cannot tell whether a model judges the building itself or relies on its neighbourhood as a shortcut, and this study tests which of the two current models do. We stratify buildings by their agreement with their neighbours, move buildings into new neighbourhoods with their pixels unchanged, and diagnose nine models covering the main paradigms of BDA. All models miss a third to half of the isolated damaged buildings, and in non-flood events they call 73--87% of intact buildings damaged once these are moved into a damaged neighbourhood. We propose a building-evidence plug-in that adds two experts to an existing model without retraining it. One expert sees only the building, and the other sees its surroundings but is trained with seamlessly swapped neighbourhoods so that it learns not to trust them. With each of the nine models, the plug-in maintains or improves overall accuracy, reduces errors on buildings that disagree with their neighbours by 8--18%, and cuts by 40--69% the intact buildings that a damaged neighbourhood turns into damaged ones. It also raises the detection of synthetic isolated damage from 1--6% to 13--39% and carries over to optical--SAR data and to an unseen earthquake. High overall scores thus conceal a systematic reliance on the neighbourhood, which building-level evidence reduces at low cost.

</details>


### [23] [Complementing Text Based Moderation with Multimodal Grounding: Detecting Fraudulent Inducement Behaviors on Short Video Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38185)
*Xiaoyong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OCR+MLLM 的多模态审核方案用于短视频金融黑灰产引流检测，在自建 2,419 条视频数据集上实现更优的时间定位（mIoU 26.52 vs 14.49）并保持 67.0% 宏召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台成为互联网金融黑灰产主要引流渠道（点赞、关注、私聊、外链等诱导），视频短、动态、诱导意图常通过瞬时视觉线索而非稳定文本表达。现有 OCR+LLM 逐帧转写并分类，仅利用文本信息，无法捕捉 OCR 不可见的视觉诱导；基于关键帧的定位也无法恢复精确时间边界。系统需同时保证检测召回与违规片段定位，使人工复核仅查看相关片段。

Method: 提出 OCR+MLLM：保留 OCR 文本通路以保障召回，由多模态大模型联合完成 OCR 接地的判别与时间定位（grounding），覆盖文本与非文本诱导。对比三条路线：OCR+LLM、OCR+MLLM、大规模零样本 MLLM，从检测、时延、token 消耗、部署复杂度四维度评估。构建含 2,419 条互联网金融短视频（66.49 小时）、3,194 个标注事件、四类别的数据集，并研究类别不平衡下 RL 定位的奖励设计。

Result: 两项关键发现：辅助奖励项会被奖励黑客（reward hacking）利用；更强的定位奖励反而损害类别均衡检测性能。据此设计召回加权 F2 定位奖励并结合类别均衡重采样。实验显示 OCR+MLLM 定位优于关键帧 OCR+LLM 基线（mIoU 26.52 vs. 14.49），可检出纯文本流水线遗漏的非文本诱导，同时保持 67.0% 宏召回。

Conclusion: OCR 提供的文本信号与 MLLM 的多模态判别和 grounding 能力互补，是兼顾召回保障与精确片段定位的可行路径；RL 奖励设计需警惕奖励黑客与检测-定位的权衡，采用召回加权 F2 奖励加类别均衡重采样可缓解该冲突。

Abstract: Short video platforms have become a primary channel for black and gray industry operations in internet finance, where inducements such as prompts to like, follow, join private chat groups, or click external links frequently steer users toward fraud. Because such videos are short, dynamic, and often convey intent through fleeting visual cues rather than stable text, a moderation system must guarantee detection recall and localize the offending segment so reviewers inspect only the relevant clip. Existing OCR+LLM pipelines transcribe on-screen text frame by frame and classify the transcript, but exploit only textual information. Many inducements are conveyed through visual cues OCR cannot observe, and keyframe-based localization cannot recover precise temporal boundaries. We explore OCR+MLLM, which augments the text pipeline with a Multimodal Large Language Model, and compare three routes: OCR+LLM, OCR+MLLM, and a large zero-shot MLLM, along detection, latency, token consumption, and deployment complexity. OCR preserves the text signal that guarantees recall, while the MLLM jointly performs OCR-grounded discrimination and temporal grounding, localizing both textual and non-textual inducements. We construct a dataset of 2,419 internet finance short videos (66.49 hours) with 3,194 annotated events across four categories, and study reward design for RL-based grounding under class imbalance. We report two findings: auxiliary reward terms are exploited through reward hacking, and stronger localization rewards degrade class-balanced detection. We therefore design a recall-weighted F2 localization reward with class-balanced resampling. Experiments show OCR+MLLM localizes better than the keyframe OCR+LLM baseline (mIoU 26.52 vs. 14.49) and detects non-textual inducements that text-only pipelines miss, while maintaining 67.0 percent macro recall.

</details>


### [24] [Silhouette-Based Gaitomics for Frailty Assessment in Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38223)
*Laura McDaniel,Ryan Roemmich,Crystal Szczesny,Peter Abadir,Rama Chellappa,Basudha Pal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出基于剪影的“步态组学”(gaitomics)框架，用可解释的视频步态特征预测Fried衰弱表型，并将模型预测归因到具体的身体构型与运动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 步行可反映身体机能，视频步态有望支持可解释的衰弱评估；但需要把衰弱预测与可测量的身体构型/运动模式建立联系，而非黑箱输出。

Method: 66名老年人，Fried衰弱表型评估；标准化剪影与步态能量图生成90个形状、时间变化、运动、纹理描述符；采用互信息差准则的mRMR进行特征选择；嵌套分层交叉验证（外层5折、内层3折，按受试者划分），特征排序与特征数选择仅限训练集；用SHAP分析对留出预测的贡献。

Result: 双侧剪影差异在三分类及两项涉及衰弱者的比较中居综合特征排序首位；归因谱随任务而异，三分类中纹理与相邻帧剪影差异突出。CatBoost三分类平均micro-AUC最高为0.647±0.085；成对比较中衰弱vs非衰弱0.833±0.249、衰弱vs衰弱前期0.765±0.168、衰弱前期vs非衰弱0.554±0.129。

Conclusion: 该方法将预测与显式步态特征相连，揭示任务依赖的特征优先级，并显示衰弱前期与非衰弱区分能力有限；框架可作为可解释研究基础，尚需更大规模独立研究验证可靠性与临床价值。

Abstract: Walking offers a window into physical function that may support interpretable video-based frailty assessment. We developed silhouette-based "gaitomics" to connect frailty predictions with measurable patterns of body configuration and movement. In 66 older adults assessed using the Fried Frailty Phenotype, standardized silhouettes and gait energy images yielded 90 descriptors of shape, temporal variation, motion, and texture. Minimum redundancy maximum relevance selection using the mutual information difference criterion prioritized informative features while limiting redundancy. Each classifier was evaluated using nested stratified cross-validation with five outer and three inner folds at the participant level, with feature ranking and feature-count selection confined to training partitions. Shapley additive explanations examined contributions to held-out predictions. Bilateral silhouette difference led the aggregate feature-selection rankings for three-class classification and both comparisons involving frail participants. Attribution profiles differed across tasks, with texture and consecutive-frame silhouette differences prominent in three-class classification. Among classifier-specific evaluations, CatBoost achieved the highest mean three-class micro-AUC of 0.647 \pm 0.085. Leading pairwise mean micro-AUCs were 0.833 \pm 0.249 for frail versus nonfrail, 0.765 \pm 0.168 for frail versus prefrail, and 0.554 \pm 0.129 for prefrail versus nonfrail classification. These findings connect model predictions to explicit gait characteristics while revealing task-dependent feature priorities and limited separation of prefrail and nonfrail participants. The framework provides an interpretable basis for further investigation, with larger independent studies needed to establish reliability and clinical relevance.

</details>


### [25] [MEV: A Multi-Event Video Dataset for Long-Take Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38241)
*Peiyuan Zhu,Shaoan Xie,Yifan Shen,Wen Tian,Zijian Li,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向长镜头视频生成的多事件视频数据集MEV，含精确时间边界与细粒度流程标注，并配套标注流水线与事件中心评测协议，微调后提升长镜头事件切换与内容连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频数据集多为短片段、单事件或粗粒度描述，对长镜头时序依赖的监督不足，在描述多事件时生成结果时序不一致。

Method: 构建MEV数据集：真实高质量视频，主体执行一系列互不重叠的独立事件，具有精确时间边界与细粒度流程标注；以事件为中心进行离散化以降低混淆因素，支持时间局部化条件控制，由原子事件单元组合成长镜头。同时设计标注流水线，将视频切分为时间对齐事件并生成全局级与事件级结构化描述；在事件中心评测协议下，利用带时间对齐事件条件的MEV微调模型进行多事件生成实验。

Result: 微调后长镜头事件过渡更优、内容更连贯，并验证了事件中心评测协议的有效性。

Conclusion: MEV为多事件微调与事件中心评测提供公开资源，并为从时间结构化视频-文本数据中学习提供实践性洞见。

Abstract: Current datasets for text-to-video generation are largely limited to short clips depicting a single event or coarse-grained captions. As a result, they provide limited supervision for modeling long-take temporal dependencies, often leading to temporally inconsistent generations when describing multiple events. In this paper, we introduce MEV, a multi-event video dataset designed for long-take video generation. MEV provides high-quality real-world videos where subjects can perform a sequence of distinct, non-overlapping events, featuring precise temporal boundaries and fine-grained procedural annotations. This event-centric discretization reduces confounding factors and enables models to support temporally localized conditioning and build long-take videos from atomic event units. Furthermore, we introduce a comprehensive annotation pipeline that segments videos into temporally grounded events, and generates structured captions at both the global level and the event level. In addition, under an event-centric evaluation protocol, we conduct multi-event generation experiments by fine-tuning on MEV with temporally grounded event conditioning, leading to improved long-take event transitions and more coherent content. We believe this work provides a publicly available resource for multi-event fine-tuning and event-centric evaluation, together with practical insights into learning from temporally structured video-text data.

</details>


### [26] [ETA-Motion: Efficient Topology-Agnostic Motion Diffusion via Sparse Joint Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38259)
*Seoyoung Hwang,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ETA-Motion 提出一种基于稀疏关节学习的拓扑无关运动生成框架，仅用紧凑关节子集训练生成器，通过语义关节嵌入与拓扑感知训练目标实现跨骨架对应，并在推理时支持全骨架生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动生成假设固定骨架模板，而拓扑无关设置需处理不同角色的多样关节配置与不一致的关节语义。现有方法采用稠密全关节建模，计算开销大，且阻碍跨不同骨架拓扑的可迁移运动模式学习。

Method: 从全部关节中选取紧凑关节子集以减少冗余，在稀疏骨架上训练运动生成器；引入融合文本与视觉线索的语义关节嵌入以建立跨拓扑对应；设计拓扑感知训练目标以保持骨架结构。训练仅用稀疏骨架观测，推理时支持全骨架运动生成。

Result: 实验表明 ETA-Motion 优于现有拓扑无关基线，同时显著提升效率，并对未见运动与未见拓扑具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 稀疏关节学习是解决拓扑无关运动生成中计算冗余与跨拓扑迁移难题的有效途径，可在不牺牲全骨架生成能力的前提下兼顾效率与泛化性。

Abstract: Topology-agnostic motion generation aims to synthesize motions for diverse characters with arbitrary skeletons. Unlike conventional motion generation, which assumes a fixed skeletal template, this setting must handle diverse joint configurations and inconsistent joint semantics across characters. Existing methods address this challenge through dense full-joint modeling, which incurs substantial computational cost and hinders learning transferable motion patterns across diverse skeletal topologies. We present ETA-Motion, a topology-agnostic motion generation framework based on sparse joint learning. Instead of learning motion from all joints, our method uses a compact subset of joints to reduce redundancy and trains the motion generator on the resulting sparse skeleton. To facilitate correspondence across diverse topologies, we introduce semantic joint embeddings that combine textual and visual cues, together with topology-aware training objectives for preserving skeletal structure. Although ETA-Motion is trained only on sparse skeletal observations, it supports full-skeleton motion generation during inference. Experimental results demonstrate that ETA-Motion outperforms existing topology-agnostic baselines while significantly improving efficiency and generalization to unseen motions and unseen topologies.

</details>


### [27] [TreemapMix: Dirichlet-Controlled Multi-Image Augmentation for Probability and Ordinal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38265)
*Ejafa Bassam,Konstantin Garbers,Yingsheng Geng,Dalton Jens,Jiaxu Qian,Guyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TreemapMix：用 Dirichlet 分布采样多图混合权重，并以 treemap 分区实现面积比例可控的多图像数据增强；同一面积信息可分别转为软标签（SCE）或序数监督（PL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合样本增强多局限于两张图像混合或固定小布局，无法灵活控制源图像数量、可见面积比例及由此诱导的监督信号。

Method: 从 Dirichlet 分布采样各源图像的权重，通过 treemap 划分将其映射为面积成比例的区域，得到多图合成样本；基于已知区域面积构建两种目标：TreemapMix-SCE 将面积作为软类别概率目标，TreemapMix-PL 采用 Plackett-Luce 目标将面积转化为序数监督。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 同配方训练下，TreemapMix-SCE 的校准误差显著低于所评估的混合样本基线，TreemapMix-PL 取得最高 Top-1 精度；目标检测与实例分割迁移实验表明预训练特征在分类之外仍有价值。

Conclusion: 受控的多图合成可将同一区域面积信息分别用于概率匹配或排序匹配监督，二者各有优势，验证了面积可控混合的有效性。

Abstract: Data augmentation improves visual recognition by exposing models to synthetic training examples that encourage generalization beyond the observed data. Mixed-sample methods extend this idea by combining multiple images. However, many common approaches either mix only pairs of images or rely on fixed small layouts, limiting control over source count, visible area proportions, and induced supervision. We propose TreemapMix, a distribution-controlled multi-image augmentation that samples source weights from a Dirichlet distribution and assigns them to area-proportional regions using a treemap partition. The resulting known region areas support two complementary objectives. TreemapMix with soft cross-entropy (SCE) treats areas as soft class-probability targets, while TreemapMix with a Plackett-Luce (PL) objective converts areas into ordinal supervision. On ImageNet-1K under matched training recipes, TreemapMix-SCE achieves substantially lower calibration error than evaluated mixed-sample baselines, while TreemapMix-PL achieves the strongest Top-1 accuracy. Transfer experiments on object detection and instance segmentation suggest that TreemapMix-pretrained features remain useful beyond classification. These results show that controlled multi-image composition can expose the same region-area information as either probability-matching or rank-matching supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/Ejafa/treemapmix.

</details>


### [28] [BudSplat: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting under a Rendering Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38278)
*Muyu Xu,Fangneng Zhan,Yu Wei,Ling Shao,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BudSplat，一个紧凑的前馈 3DGS 框架，通过细节感知重要性评分与质量感知高斯剪枝，在有限基元数量下保留最有价值的高斯，从而在渲染质量与效率之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈 3D Gaussian Splatting 从稀疏视图扩展到密集多视图输入时，候选高斯数量急剧增长；既有方法无法可靠区分冗余基元，导致重复观测区域产生大量冗余基元，渲染开销上升而视觉质量提升有限。

Method: 两项核心设计：(1) 细节感知重要性评分（detail-aware importance score），从多层级视觉 token 中提取，为剪枝可能损害感知质量的区域提供显式线索，并可注入体素融合过程，在多视图聚合时保留高价值局部贡献者；(2) 质量感知高斯剪枝（quality-aware Gaussian pruning），联合考虑渲染贡献与细节重要性来选择紧凑基元集合，并支持局部配额重分配，避免向大规模冗余区域过度分配基元。

Result: 在 RE10K、DL3DV 与 ACID 数据集上的实验表明，BudSplat 在渲染质量与渲染效率之间实现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: BudSplat 通过在受限基元预算下学习保留最有价值的高斯，有效缓解了密集多视图前馈 3DGS 的冗余与渲染效率瓶颈，验证了重要性引导融合与剪枝的有效性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently emerged as a promising alternative to per-scene optimization, enabling fast novel view synthesis by predicting Gaussian primitives directly from input images. However, it quickly faces a bottleneck of rendering efficiency while scaling from sparse to dense multi-view inputs, which offer more geometric constraints but produce a rapidly growing set of candidate Gaussians. Existing solutions cannot reliably distinguish redundant primitives, leading to many redundant primitives from repeatedly observed regions and increased rendering cost without much gain in visual quality. We propose BudSplat, a compact feed-forward 3DGS framework that learns to keep the most valuable Gaussians under a limited primitive count. BudSplat has two novel designs. The first is a detail-aware importance score that is extracted from multi-level visual tokens, providing an explicit cue for regions where pruning is likely to harm perceptual quality. The score can be injected into voxel fusion to preserve high-value local contributors during multi-view aggregation. The second is quality-aware Gaussian pruning that selects a compact set of primitives by jointly considering rendering contribution and detail importance. The quality-aware pruning enables local quota redistribution that helps avoid over-allocating primitives to large redundant regions. Experiments on RE10K, DL3DV, and ACID show that BudSplat achieves a favorable trade-off among rendering quality and efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [ConvRoute-ViT: Convolutional Token Routing for Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38284)
*Manish Das,Shiv Ram Dubey,Satish Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ConvRoute-ViT，一种基于卷积路由的 token 缩减机制：保留类别注意力高的 patch token，其余 token 经空间重组与卷积压缩为摘要 token，从而降低序列长度而不完全丢弃上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 自注意力复杂度随 token 数二次增长，计算开销大；标准 ViT 对所有层、所有 patch token 同等对待，导致冗余计算并削弱对最具判别性区域的关注。

Method: 在选定层依据 class-token 注意力保留最相关的 patch token；将剩余 token 进行空间重组并压缩为紧凑的卷积摘要 token；分层（hierarchically）应用该路由过程，为深层构建紧凑且具判别性的表示。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 与 TinyImageNet 上，与基础 ViT 相比，ConvRoute-ViT 在保持分类精度的同时提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过卷积路由实现的分层 token 缩减可在不牺牲精度的前提下降低 ViT 计算成本，说明保留部分上下文信息的压缩式 token 缩减优于简单丢弃 token。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong image recognition performance, but their self-attention increases quadratically with the number of input tokens and makes them computationally expensive. The standard implementation of ViT models treats all patch tokens with equal importance across every layer of the model. This uniform treatment introduces redundant computation and may reduce the model's focus on the most discriminative object regions. In this paper, we propose ConvRoute-ViT, a convolutional token routing mechanism for efficient vision transformers that performs token reduction through convolutional routing. Our model preserves the most relevant patch tokens according to class-token attention at selected layers, while the remaining tokens are spatially reorganized and compressed into a compact set of convolutional summary tokens. This design reduces the sequence length without completely removing the contextual information of the less informative tokens. By applying the routing process hierarchically, the proposed framework constructs compact and discriminative representations for deeper layers. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet show that ConvRoute-ViT improves computational efficiency while maintaining classification accuracy compared with the base ViT.

</details>


### [30] [PulmoWorld: An Asynchronous Multimodal Medical World Model for Longitudinal Pneumonia Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38289)
*Cheng Tang,Xinyu Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Minghui Zhang,Shaocong Wei,Wei Li,Yuxing Chen,Kongcheng Zhang,Chun-Mei Feng,Nanqing Dong,Xinyuan Zhu,Ziyan Huang,Junjun He,Yirong Chen,Lingtong Huang,Min Cen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PULMOWORLD 是一个面向纵向肺炎动态的世界模型，统一了异步状态估计、递归模拟与证据约束规划，在 MASS 队列上实现与任务专用序列模型相当的递归预测，并保留预后信息，Cap-MBRL 在合成环境中降低选择遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺炎涉及呼吸、炎症与器官功能多维度变化，其测量在时间上异步到达且常不完整，现有方法难以在信息稀疏、观测时点不规则的条件下统一建模状态演化并支撑临床决策规划。

Method: 提出 PULMOWORLD 世界模型：1) 历史感知滤波将状态传播与观测驱动校正解耦，仅在观测出现时更新共享信念；2) 基于该信念递归预测临床数值与模态表示及未来预后摘要；3) Cap-MBRL 在授权动作集内搜索想象轨迹，仅当候选满足风险预算与预测代价裕度时替换冻结的兜底策略。模型分别在公开 MIMIC-IV-CXR 胸片与临床轨迹上训练。

Result: 在肺炎队列 MASS 上，递归预测与任务训练序列模型具有竞争力，模拟未来状态保留预后信息；在锁定肺炎面板上，预后区分度优于最新值融合，长期预测误差具竞争力；在五个已知机制的 MASS-Syn 环境中，Cap-MBRL 相较直接执行兜底策略降低选择遗憾。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将异步状态估计、递归世界模型模拟与证据约束规划相结合，可在观测不完整、时序不规则的肺炎场景中同时支撑预测与决策，为纵向临床世界模型提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Pneumonia changes across respiration, inflammation, and organ function, while the measurements that describe it arrive at different times and are often incomplete. We introduce PULMOWORLD, a world model for longitudinal pneumonia dynamics that connects asynchronous state estimation, recursive simulation, and evidence-constrained planning. History-aware filtering separates state propagation from observation-driven correction, so a measurement updates the shared belief only when it is observed. That belief supports recursive forecasts of clinical values and modality representations, and summaries of future prognosis. Cap-MBRL searches imagined continuations inside an authorized action set and replaces a frozen fallback only when a candidate meets the risk budget and a predicted-cost margin. On the pneumonia cohort MASS, recursive forecasts are competitive with task-trained sequence models, and simulated future states retain prognostic information. Trained separately on public MIMIC-IV-CXR chest radiographs and clinical trajectories, this model improves prognostic discrimination over latest-value fusion in development and is competitive on long-horizon error on the locked pneumonia panel. In five known-mechanism MASS-Syn environments, Cap-MBRL lowers selection regret relative to direct fallback execution.

</details>


### [31] [Hidden Commitment: When Language Models Silently Pick a Side and How Steering Can Surface It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38296)
*Samuel Dawit Assefa,Jae Won Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冲突上下文的问答任务中，语言模型的答案并未如实反映其内部决策：Qwen 0.8B 的残差流中存在一个"来源选择方向"，其符号决定答案采用哪份文档，且可直接调控；而"冲突披露"是较弱、较晚层、仅部分可操控的另一机制。据此提出用残差读出监测隐性来源承诺、并配合披露引导的检索系统监控策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文挑战一个普遍假设——语言模型的输出可视为其内部决策的可见记录。在提供两份互相矛盾文档的冲突语境问答中，模型可能已处理了分歧却仍给出一边倒的答案，即使提示要求其披露冲突。因此需要探究：模型内部是否存在可测量的"隐性来源承诺"，以及它与"冲突披露"是否由不同机制控制，从而为检索增强系统提供监控手段。

Method: 1) 构建冲突上下文 QA 设定：两份文档支持互不相容的答案，并在部分提示中明确要求披露冲突；2) 在 Qwen 0.8B 的残差流中定位"来源选择方向"，以符号判定答案实际采用哪份来源，并检验其与行为的对应关系；3) 分析中层稀疏 MLP writer 是否向同一坐标写入信息；4) 通过 steering 干预该坐标，检验能否改变所选来源；5) 用 LLM 审核标签在 Gemma 3 1B 上做迁移实验，检验读出与稀疏写入桥的可复现性；6) 对比分析冲突披露在层深、强度与可操控性上的差异模式。

Result: 在 Qwen 0.8B 中，残差来源选择方向的符号可靠追踪答案使用的文档；中层稀疏 MLP writer 向同一坐标写入，steering 该坐标可改变来源选择。Gemma 3 1B 的迁移实验在 LLM 审核标签下保留了来源选择读出与稀疏写入桥。冲突披露则表现为更弱、位于更后层、且仅部分可 steering，与来源承诺机制明显分离。

Conclusion: 模型的单边答案不等于其内部决策的完整披露，隐性来源承诺是可读出、可干预的内部变量，并与冲突披露解耦。实践上建议：检索系统用残差读出检测静默来源承诺，并在模型于分歧下已选边时，将冲突呈现提示与披露 steering 结合使用。

Abstract: Language-model answers are often treated as a visible record of the internal decision that produced them. We test this assumption in conflicting-context question answering, where two supplied documents support incompatible answers. A model can process the disagreement and still return a one-sided answer, even under prompts that ask it to disclose conflict. In Qwen 0.8B, hidden source commitment is captured by a residual source-choice direction whose sign tracks which document the answer uses. Sparse middle-layer MLP writers feed the same coordinate, and steering the coordinate changes the chosen source. A transfer run on Gemma 3 1B preserves the source-choice readout and sparse-write bridge under LLM-audited labels. Conflict disclosure follows a different pattern, appearing weaker, later-layer, and only partially steerable. These results support a practical monitoring strategy for retrieval systems that detects silent source commitment with residual readouts and pairs conflict-surfacing prompts with disclosure steering when the model has selected a side under disagreement.

</details>


### [32] [Don't Ask the LLM: Visual Token Pruning with Spectral Structure and Semantic Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38303)
*Intaek Shin,Geuntaek Lim,Sungjune Park,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 ST-Pruner，在进入 LLM 前结合谱显著性与文本相似度进行视觉 token 剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 将图像/视频表示为长视觉 token 序列，推理开销大；视觉侧 [CLS] 注意力在激进压缩下选择分散且偏背景，而查询条件信号需在 LLM 内大量计算后才可得，故希望在咨询 LLM 之前完成结构化、查询感知的 token 选择。

Method: ST-Pruner（Spectral-Textual Pruner）训练无关框架：将注意力无关的谱显著性（spectral saliency）与反转的 patch-text 相似度（inverted patch-text similarity）通过无参数乘性融合相结合，实现 pre-LLM 视觉 token 选择。

Result: 在多种 LVLM 上，仅用不超过 11.1% 的视觉 token 即保留 95% 以上的全 token 性能；在 LLaVA-NeXT 上实现 3.2 倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 结构化、查询感知的视觉 token 选择可在 LLM 之前有效完成，谱与文本信号的乘性融合为高效 LVLM 推理提供了免训练方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) represent images and videos as long visual-token sequences, substantially increasing inference costs. These costs motivate visual-token pruning, whose effectiveness depends on both what signal is used for selection and when that signal becomes available. Our analysis reveals complementary limitations: visual-side [CLS] attention produces scattered, background-biased selections under aggressive compression, while stronger query-conditioned signals become available after substantial computation within the large language model (LLM). This raises a natural question: can structurally coherent, query-aware token selection be performed before consulting the LLM? We therefore propose ST-Pruner (Spectral-Textual Pruner), a training-free framework for pre-LLM visual-token selection. It combines attention-independent spectral saliency with inverted patch-text similarity through parameter-free multiplicative fusion. Across diverse LVLMs, ST-Pruner retains over 95% of full-token performance using at most 11.1% of visual tokens and achieves a 3.2x end-to-end speedup on LLaVA-NeXT. Code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/ST-Pruner.

</details>


### [33] [Does 3D Pose Improve Action Valuation in Football? An Empirical Study of Pose Representations and Learning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38308)
*Rikuhei Umemoto,Kenjiro Ide,Soham Majumder,Ulf Brefeld,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究将3D姿态加入基于SARSA的足球动作价值评估模型，发现姿态能改善临近动作预测（AUC提升），但在50组对比中仅3组稳健提升动作价值（回报预测），而训练协议（是否使用动作预测损失）的影响远大于姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球多智能体深度强化学习（MADRL）动作价值模型仅使用2D位置与速度，忽略了与即将发生动作相关的姿态信息。作者据此检验：在SARSA框架中加入3D姿态是否能提升观测动作价值相对于进攻回报（进球、失球或最终期望控球价值）的估计精度。

Method: 使用UEFA欧冠2022/23的事件、追踪与3D姿态数据，比较六种表示（无姿态、原始关节、躯干与头部朝向、ST-GCN嵌入等）在五种学习设置与三个随机种子的表现。学习设置变化时序差分目标与动作预测损失，并以蒙特卡洛回报回归作为参照，共进行50组有球/无球对比。

Result: 仅3组对比中姿态稳健提升回报预测，均为躯干与头部朝向表示；12组反而变差，多为ST-GCN。训练选择影响更大：去除动作预测损失使仅位置价值的在有球技能从0.003升至0.040，与期望进球相关性从0.23升至0.60，且改善在等训练时长下仍存在。姿态显著提升0.2 s后动作预测（宏AUC 0.914→0.934，原始关节）以及传球成功预测（AUC 0.844→0.856）。

Conclusion: 在该训练协议下，姿态信息有助于刻画临近动作，但很少改善观测动作价值的估计；相比姿态表示，训练目标设计（如动作预测损失）是更关键的因素。

Abstract: Football action valuation estimates players' contributions to future scoring. Existing multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MADRL) models use 2D positions and velocities, omitting posture information relevant to imminent actions. We investigate whether adding 3D pose to a SARSA-based model improves observed-action values relative to attack returns: goals, conceded goals or final expected possession value. Using UEFA Champions League 2022/23 event, tracking and 3D pose data, we compare six representations (no pose, raw joints, torso and head orientation, and spatial-temporal graph convolutional network (ST-GCN) embeddings) across five learning settings and three seeds. The settings vary temporal-difference targets and action-prediction loss, with Monte Carlo return regression as a reference. Across 50 on-ball and off-ball comparisons, pose robustly improved return prediction in only three, all using torso and head orientation, while worsening it in twelve, mostly with ST-GCN. Training choices had greater effects: removing the action-prediction loss raised on-ball skill of position-only values from 0.003 to 0.040 and their correlation with expected goals from 0.23 to 0.60; the improvement in return prediction persisted at matched training length. Separately, pose improved prediction of the on-ball action 0.2 s ahead (macro AUC 0.914 to 0.934 with raw joints), while the passer's pose during execution improved pass-success prediction (AUC 0.844 to 0.856). Within this training protocol, pose informed imminent actions but rarely improved observed-action values.

</details>


### [34] [Extremely Sparse-View Computed Tomography from 2D Projections via Pose-Aware Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38319)
*Linrui Dai,Jinxiu Liang,Chu Zhou,Mae Yamaguchi,Ryuya Takahashi,Imari Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究“训练稀疏/推理稀疏”(train-sparse/infer-sparse)场景下的极端稀疏视角CT重建:从每样本仅一张2D投影学习位姿感知扩散先验,并在采样中用测量保真、共享体重投影与投影物理一致性引导,实现优于稠密体监督基线的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端稀疏视角CT(xSV-CT)中,扫描时间、运动或辐射限制使每例仅几张投影,同样限制数据集构建,难以获得稠密扫描预重建3D体。现有方法存在缺口:优化类方法直接用稀疏测量但缺乏数据驱动先验,难以抑制伪影;学习类方法先验强但通常依赖xSV-CT范围之外的稠密扫描训练体。因此需要研究训练与推理均稀疏的设定。

Method: 从每个训练样本的单张2D投影学习位姿感知(pose-aware)扩散先验;在扩散采样过程中引入三类引导:测量视角保真度、共享体重投影(shared-volume reprojection)以及投影物理一致性(projection-physics consistency),用于断层重建。

Result: 在两个公开CT数据集、1/4/8视角设置下,该方法取得最佳新视角合成分数,重建质量优于或可比于使用稠密体监督的基线。

Conclusion: 表明无需稠密扫描训练体,仅凭单张投影即可学习有效先验并结合物理一致性引导,在训练与推理均稀疏的条件下实现高质量极端稀疏视角CT重建。

Abstract: Extremely sparse-view computed tomography (xSV-CT) is critical when scanning time, motion, or radiation tolerance limits each scan to only a few projections.
