Method: 构建显式统计模型：(1) 归一化卷积（normalized convolution）在像素与颜色通道上对高斯场做空间与通道相关建模；(2) 查找表将每个高斯值在给定干净强度条件下映射为噪声残差。由于底层场保持标准高斯分布，两个组件可分别独立拟合。

Result: 同一套公式同时适用于 SIDD 相机噪声与 Newson 模拟胶片颗粒。在 SIDD 上取得所评估方法中最低的平均分布误差与相关误差，在胶片颗粒上保持竞争力；用其合成噪声训练的降噪器最接近用真实噪声训练的效果；GPU 上渲染一帧 1080p 仅需 10 ms。

Conclusion: 显式统计建模可作为物理模型与神经生成器之间的有效折衷：拟合简单、可分离、跨噪声类型通用，合成噪声的训练价值与真实噪声接近，且推理高效，适合作为噪声合成与降噪训练的实用方案。

Abstract: Noise synthesis is commonly approached through physical models of image formation or neural networks trained to generate noisy images. We explore a middle ground: an explicit statistical model fitted directly from paired clean and noisy images. A normalized convolution correlates a Gaussian field across pixels and color channels, and a lookup maps each Gaussian value to a residual given the clean intensity. Because the field stays standard Gaussian, the two components are fitted separately, without reproducing the physical process or training a neural generator. With one formulation for camera noise from SIDD and simulated film grain from Newson, the model has the lowest average distribution and correlation errors on SIDD among the evaluated methods and remains competitive on film grain. A denoiser trained on its noise comes closest to one trained on real noise, and a 1080p frame renders in 10 ms on GPU.

</details>


### [139] [What Survives Label Downsampling? Exact Region Survival for Coarse Segmentation Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39880)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Yukun Ou,San Tek Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MatchDown，用区域与网格单元之间的最小代价匹配来构造分割网络的粗粒度目标标签，替代传统的跨步采样、最近邻重采样或多数池化下采样，以最大限度保留小区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下采样方法（strided sampling、nearest-neighbor、majority pooling）对每个粗网格单元独立标注，导致未在任一单元中占优的区域从目标中完全消失，得不到正标签，小目标/稀有区域被系统性丢弃。

Method: MatchDown将粗目标构建建模为区域与所占据网格单元之间的最小代价二部匹配；可证明保留任何“每单元一区域”目标所能保留的最大区域数，并给出不可避免损失的数量。扩展版MatchDown-P引入优先级分层，保证每个被评估区域至少获得一个单元面积。

Result: 在COCO、Cityscapes、BBBC038上因子2–16下，MatchDown-P恰好达到最小损失数；COCO因子8时传统方法（StarDist、nnU-Net、连接组金字塔）损失量为4–5倍。BBBC038上星凸头用MatchDown目标可分割出35%被多数池化丢失且IoU>0.5的核（多数目标15%、StarDist跨步24%）。两核数据集上Acc50比跨步目标高2.6–4.2点，较cell-center采样低至多2.8点；BBBC039上仿射头计数误差较多数目标降低9.3个核/图但Acc50低7.2点。

Conclusion: 保留区域于目标中与重建其形状是两个不同问题：被多数池化丢失的区域无法通过上采样任何粗目标恢复到IoU>0.5，但基于MatchDown训练的head仍能分割出相当部分此类区域。MatchDown作为训练前的标签下采样替代步骤，一次运行即可，能更保真地保留实例。

Abstract: Segmentation networks that predict at an output stride are trained against label maps downsampled by strided sampling, nearest-neighbor resampling or majority pooling. These methods label each coarse grid cell on its own, so a region that wins no cell is absent from the target and receives no positive label. We present MatchDown, which builds the coarse target from a minimum-cost matching between regions and the grid cells they occupy. It keeps as many regions as any one-region-per-cell target can, certifies how many losses are unavoidable, and with priority tiers (MatchDown-P) keeps every evaluated region (a region that a metric scores) of at least one cell's area. On COCO, Cityscapes and BBBC038 at factors 2 to 16, MatchDown-P loses exactly the minimum number of regions, whereas the downsampling used by StarDist, nnU-Net and connectomics pyramids loses four to five times as many on COCO at factor 8. Keeping a region in the target and reconstructing its shape are different problems: we show that no region lost by majority pooling can be reconstructed above IoU 0.5 by upsampling any coarse target, yet on BBBC038 a star-convex head trained on MatchDown targets segments 35% of the nuclei that majority pooling loses above IoU 0.5, against 15% when trained on majority targets and 24% on StarDist's strided target. On two nucleus datasets, with thresholds cross-validated for count error, this head has instance accuracy at IoU 0.5 (Acc50) 2.6 to 4.2 points above the strided target and at most 2.8 points below cell-center sampling, and a lower count error than the strided and cell-center targets on BBBC039. On BBBC039, an affinity head, which outputs one label per cell, lowers count error relative to majority targets by 9.3 nuclei per image at 7.2 points lower Acc50. MatchDown runs once, before training, in place of the label-downsampling step.

</details>


### [140] [sDMD: Sinkhorn Distribution Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39889)
*Jaihoon Kim,Lingxiao Li,Soshi Shimada,Roshni Cooper,Minhyuk Sung,Yiwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将DMD与Wasserstein梯度流联系，指出其在积分KL能量基础上引入最优传输项（Sinkhorn散度）以修正低密度区不可靠的教师评分与模式坍缩问题，并提出判别器自适应激活Sinkhorn修正的sDMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD通过最小化反向KL将多步扩散模型蒸馏为少步生成器，但仅依赖局部密度引导：低教师密度区域的教师分数不可靠，且反向KL的模式寻求特性会使学生坍缩到教师的部分模式。作者希望同时解决离群样本引导与模式覆盖问题。

Method: 首先将DMD联系到Wasserstein梯度流，证明其梯度源于以积分KL（IKL）为能量泛函的不动点表述；随后在IKL上增广一个最优传输项，用有限批次估计的Sinkhorn散度实现，将离群样本拉向目标并促进模式覆盖。针对有限批次下Sinkhorn速度估计误差会扰动IKL引导已准确的域内样本，引入sDMD：依据判别器输出自适应激活Sinkhorn修正，仅作用于IKL可能不可靠的离群样本。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev、Qwen-Image-20B与Wan2.1-14B上以2–4步采样，sDMD在GenEval、DPG、WISE与VBench上优于蒸馏基线，且相比DMD2提升了样本多样性。

Conclusion: 将DMD解释为IKL能量下的Wasserstein梯度流，并以Sinkhorn散度增广、经判别器自适应门控，可在少步蒸馏中兼顾质量、指令遵循与多样性，为DMD类方法提供了新理论视角与实用改进。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) distills multi-step diffusion models into few-step generators by minimizing reverse KL divergence. Despite its strong performance, DMD guides samples by local density alone, which introduces two limitations: teacher scores can be unreliable in regions of low teacher density, and the mode-seeking behavior of reverse KL can collapse the student onto a subset of the teacher modes. We first connect DMD to Wasserstein gradient flow, showing that its gradient follows from a fixed-point formulation with integral KL (IKL) as the energy functional. This perspective motivates augmenting IKL with an optimal transport term that addresses these limitations by guiding outlier samples toward the target and promoting mode coverage. We realize this term using Sinkhorn divergence estimated from finite batches. In practice, however, errors in the estimated Sinkhorn velocity can perturb in-domain samples for which IKL guidance is already accurate. To this end, we introduce sDMD, which adaptively activates the Sinkhorn correction based on discriminator outputs, focusing on outlier samples where IKL may be unreliable. With 2--4 sampling steps on FLUX.1-dev, Qwen-Image-20B, and Wan2.1-14B, sDMD outperforms distillation baselines on GenEval, DPG, WISE, and VBench while improving sample diversity over DMD2.

</details>


### [141] [TokenRepel: Generating Diverse Image Sets Without Compromising Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39913)
*Qingtao Yu,Zhen Wang,Ning Lai,Boyang Hu,Hongdong Li,Dylan Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种推理时（inference-time）方法，利用预训练文生图模型隐表示的几何结构，在同提示词下提升生成图像集的视觉多样性，同时保持平均图像质量不下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一提示词生成的图像往往缺乏多样性；希望在不重新训练、不牺牲单图保真度的前提下增加集合层面的差异。

Method: 1) 词元排斥机制：对单位超球面上特征词元的 von Mises-Fisher 混合分布取负黎曼梯度步，增大样本间特征词元夹角；2) 词元密度保持机制：将排斥引起的预测变化投影到与模型分数方向对齐的半空间，使更新停留在生成模型高密度区域；3) 将该机制扩展至各中间层输出，实现更细粒度的迭代引导。

Result: 在三类预训练文生图模型上实验，相同提示词下视觉多样性显著提升，图像保真度保持相当水平。

Conclusion: 通过隐空间几何调控可在推理阶段有效解耦“多样性—保真度”权衡，为文生图集级生成提供了一种免训练、可扩展到多层特征的通用引导方案。

Abstract: We present an inference-time approach that enhances the visual diversity of image sets generated by text-to-image models, without compromising the average image quality. Our method leverages the intrinsic geometry of hidden representations in pretrained models to diversify images generated from the same prompt. First, we introduce a token repulsion mechanism that increases the angle between feature tokens across samples. It takes a negative Riemannian gradient step on a von Mises-Fisher mixture distribution of tokens on the unit hypersphere. Second, to preserve the quality of each output image, we propose a token density preservation mechanism that projects the repulsion-induced prediction change onto a half-space aligned with the model's score direction. This encourages the updates to remain within the high-density regions learned by the generative model. We further extend this mechanism to the output of each intermediate layer, enabling finer-grained iterative guidance. We evaluate our method on three types of pretrained text-to-image models. Experiments show substantial gains in visual diversity under identical prompts, while maintaining a comparable level of image fidelity.

</details>


### [142] [PhysAg3D: Bridging 3D Generative and Agentic Models for High-Fidelity and Physically-Interactive Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39924)
*Bi'an Du,Fanpeng Meng,Jiemin Fang,Chen Yang,Pufan Li,Daizong Liu,Qi Tian,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PhysAg3D，将 3D 生成模型与视觉语言智能体的推理和工具调用结合，从单张图像生成兼具真实外观、几何保真和合理物理交互行为的 3D 资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成交互式 3D 资产需同时满足真实外观、对图像物体的保真度与合理物理行为；但高细节几何本身并不能决定物体应如何运动或形变，因此需要引入对交互与物理先验的显式建模。

Method: 1) 视觉语言智能体先从图像推断语义部件、候选交互与物理先验；2) 3D 生成器由同一图像生成带纹理的整体网格；3) 构建分层交互表示，将可修订先验与机构及其控制的生成表面关联；4) 智能体在该表示与网格观测引导下调用几何与物理工具，构建部件、关节、可形变区域与表面绑定，并保留有价值的生成几何与材质；5) 以渲染交互结果与执行测量指导固定预算内的定向修订。

Result: 在真实照片与外部基准上的定量与定性评估显示，生成的资产细节丰富、与图像对齐且交互合理；消融实验支持有界修订与源表面保持的有效性。框架同时支持刚性关节运动与柔性形变。

Conclusion: 将 3D 生成与智能体式推理和工具使用相耦合，可在保留生成质量的同时赋予单图资产可动的物理交互结构，且刚性驱动与柔性形变可统一处理。

Abstract: Generating interactive 3D assets from a single image requires realistic appearance, fidelity to the depicted object, and plausible physical behavior. Yet detailed geometry alone does not determine how an object should move or deform. We introduce PhysAg3D, which couples 3D generative models with agentic reasoning and tool use. A vision-language agent first infers semantic parts, candidate interactions, and physical priors from the image; a 3D generator then produces a textured whole-object mesh from the same image. A hierarchical interaction representation links these revisable priors to mechanisms and the generated surfaces they control, organizing interaction construction and refinement. Guided by this representation and observations of the mesh, the agent uses geometric and physical tools to construct parts, joints, deformable regions, and surface bindings while retaining useful generated geometry and materials. Rendered interactions and execution measurements guide targeted revisions within a fixed budget. The framework accommodates both rigid articulation and compliant deformation. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations on real photographs and external benchmarks demonstrate detailed, image-aligned assets with plausible interactions. Ablations support bounded refinement and source-surface preservation. Project Page: supra-3d.github.io/PhysAg3D.

</details>


### [143] [Spectral Invariant Fusion and Refinement for Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39926)
*Yansong Bai,Yunzhi Zhuge,Xinzhuo Yu,Lu Zhang,Jin Zhan,Pingping Zhang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIFR——频域感知双流框架，在融合与解码两阶段同时抑制域敏感干扰并保留细粒度空间线索，在多种DGSS基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域泛化语义分割需在训练时不接触目标域数据的条件下对未见域做稠密预测。现有方法（数据多样化、不变性学习、直接用基础模型）难以同时抑制域敏感干扰与保留细粒度空间线索。

Method: 以谱建模为统一原则，在融合与解码两阶段应对域偏移：(1) 谱引导的不变融合，替代对冻结CLIP与DINOv2特征的直接融合，滤除域敏感响应、保留互补的语义与结构信息；(2) 谱解码精炼模块，通过基于低频统计的归一化与自适应频率选择稳定多尺度解码器特征，减少残余域偏置在稠密预测中的传播。

Result: 在合成到真实、真实到真实及恶劣天气等多种DGSS设定下，稳定优于此前SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 频域建模可作为统一机制同时调控异构先验交互与稠密解码精炼，从而在严重分布偏移下联合提升域鲁棒性与分割精度。

Abstract: Domain generalized semantic segmentation is challenging because it requires accurate dense prediction on unseen domains without any access to target-domain data during training. Existing methods improve transferability through data diversification, invariant learning, or direct use of foundation models, but they often struggle to simultaneously suppress domain-sensitive interference and preserve fine-grained spatial cues. In this work, we propose SIFR, a frequency-aware dual-stream framework that tackles domain shift at both the fusion and decoding stages. SIFR uses spectral modeling as a unified principle to regulate both heterogeneous prior interaction and dense decoder refinement. Instead of directly fusing frozen CLIP and DINOv2 features, SIFR performs spectral-guided invariant fusion to filter out domain-sensitive responses while preserving complementary semantic and structural information. In addition, SIFR incorporates a spectral decoder refinement module that stabilizes multi-scale decoder features through low-frequency statistics-based normalization and adaptive frequency selection, thereby reducing the propagation of residual domain bias in dense prediction. This design enables SIFR to jointly improve domain robustness and segmentation precision under severe distribution shifts. Extensive experiments on multiple DGSS benchmarks, including synthetic-to-real, real-to-real, and adverse-weather settings, demonstrate consistent improvements over previous state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [144] [MLLM-GS: Multimodal Large Language Model Prior-Guided Fast Novel View Synthesis in Extreme Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39936)
*Zhiliang Wu,Xinye Zheng,Kun Li,Fei Wang,Hao Guo,Yanyan Wei,Guanghui Yue,Beier Zhu,Wei Zhou,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MLLM-GS：以冻结多模态大模型（MLLM）先验引导视图增强+轻量校正网络抑制像素错位与色偏，并配合优化光栅化、高斯内存布局与参数更新的高效3DGS，实现极端退化场景下的快速新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有退化场景NVS方法多依赖针对特定退化的专用模型，难以泛化到多样极端环境；生成模型先验丰富但输出存在像素错位与颜色偏差，会传播进3D重建；同时3D重建的计算与显存开销过大，限制实际部署。

Method: 双分支框架。(1) MLLM先验引导视图增强：利用冻结MLLM的丰富视觉先验增强不同严重退化下的视图，并引入轻量校正网络减少MLLM输出的像素偏移与颜色失真；仅需少量成对干净视图监督，即可为全部退化观测生成可靠增强视图，作为后续重建的伪GT。(2) 快速3D场景重建：通过改进光栅化、高斯内存布局与参数更新，减少冗余计算、访存开销与优化代价，提升3DGS效率。

Result: 在多个退化场景上的大量实验表明，MLLM-GS能有效应对多种严重退化，实现高质量NVS，同时重建时间与内存消耗显著降低。

Conclusion: MLLM先验+轻量对齐校正与高效3DGS优化的结合，为极端环境下退化场景的NVS提供了泛化性好、成本低的统一方案，兼顾增强视图可靠性与重建效率。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis (NVS) in degraded scenes aims to reconstruct 3D scenes from multi-view observations affected by various degradations, and to generate clear and photorealistic images at unseen viewpoints. Despite recent advances, existing methods often rely on degradation-specific models, limiting their generalization across diverse extreme environments. Generative models provide rich visual priors, but their outputs often suffer from pixel misalignment and color deviations, which can propagate into the reconstructed 3D scene and compromise reconstruction quality. Furthermore, the high computational and memory costs of 3D reconstruction hinder the practical application of NVS in extreme environments. To this end, we propose MLLM-GS, a multimodal large language model (MLLM) prior-guided framework for fast NVS in extreme environments. MLLM-GS consists of two branches: MLLM prior-guided view enhancement and fast 3D scene reconstruction. The former leverages the rich visual priors of a frozen MLLM to enhance views under different severe degradations and introduces a lightweight correction network to reduce pixel shifts and color distortions in MLLM outputs. With supervision from only a few paired clean views, this branch produces reliable enhanced views for all degraded observations, providing pseudo-GT views for subsequent 3D reconstruction. The latter improves 3DGS efficiency by optimizing rasterization, Gaussian memory layout, and parameter updates, reducing redundant computation, memory-access overhead, and optimization costs. Extensive experiments on multiple degraded scenes demonstrate that MLLM-GS effectively handles various severe degradations and achieves high-quality NVS with significantly lower reconstruction time and memory consumption. Project Page: https://wzlwlm13.github.io/MLLM-GS

</details>


### [145] [BiHashFormer: Hash-Driven Dual-Branch Transformer for Efficient Object Detection in HRW Shots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39937)
*Jingchen Huang,Wenxi Li,Chenyang Lyu,Moran Liu,Haozhe Lin,Yuchen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiHashFormer，一种基于哈希的双稀疏分支Transformer，用于千兆像素级高分辨率宽幅图像检测，通过RoI Selector、HashAttention与HashMiner降低前景稀疏与背景干扰带来的开销与误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千兆像素级高分辨率宽幅图像存在前景极度稀疏、分辨率极高、前景与背景交织等问题，使传统检测器与注意力机制受背景干扰，导致低效且不准确。

Method: BiHashFormer由并行双分支构成：RoI Selector预测前景比例并top-k选取含目标窗口；压缩分支处理被选窗口，将注意力细化为HashAttention，通过丢弃一半key-value对降本增效；补偿分支用HashMiner在剩余区域做低成本哈希搜索，恢复漏检目标特征。

Result: 验证HashAttention选择关系为单向性，并揭示不同query对key的偏好；在千兆像素基准PANDA上，骨干FLOPs减少37.5%，AP50提升至81.0%。

Conclusion: 并行双分支结构避免分支间依赖导致的推理延迟，稳定性显著优于单分支模型，尤其在RoI选取率较低时表现突出。

Abstract: Recent advances in gigapixel-level imaging have brought High-Resolution Wide shots to the forefront of research. However, these images present significant challenges: extreme sparsity of foreground, gigapixel-level resolutions and interleaving of foreground and background. This causes traditional detectors and attention mechanisms to be hindered by background, resulting in inefficiency and inaccuracy. To tackle this problem, we propose BiHashFormer, a dual sparsified branch transformer built on hashing. RoI Selector will predict the foreground proportion for top-k selecting target-containing windows. The compression branch processes windows selected and refines attention into HashAttention by discarding half of the key-value pairs, reducing cost and improving efficiency. The compensation branch uses HashMiner to perform low-cost hash searches in the remaining regions, recovering features of missed objects. Experiments confirm that the selection relationship in HashAttention is one-way and reveal the preference of different queries for keys. In experiments on the gigapixel benchmark PANDA, BiHashFormer reduces 37.5% backbone FLOPs while improving AP50 to 81.0%. Moreover, this parallel dual-branch structure allows the model to avoid inference delays caused by inter-branch dependencies, while achieving much greater stability than single-branch models, specially with low rates of RoI selection.

</details>


### [146] [ElasticFormer: Detecting Objects in HRW Shots via Elastic Computing Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39946)
*Wenxi Li,Jingchen Huang,Chenyang Lyu,Moran Liu,Haozhe Lin,Guiguang Ding,Yuchen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ElasticFormer：面向十亿像素级宽幅图像的自适应稀疏骨干，按前景比例动态分配算力，在PANDA上骨干FLOPs降80%且AP50显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿像素级宽幅图像存在前景极度稀疏、分辨率极高、目标数量差异大等问题，传统近景检测器被背景淹没而变得不准且慢；已有稀疏骨干采用固定稀疏模式，无法适应目标数量的变化。

Method: 提出弹性稀疏骨干ElasticFormer，依据前景比例动态分配计算资源。先基于方差对窗口打分，再由ElasticSelector模块预测前景比例并做top-k选择，引导模型选中含目标窗口、在目标聚集区域扩展算力；配套设计新损失函数与三阶段训练策略，使ElasticSelector在缺少框标注时仍可工作。另在PASCAL VOC 2007上做WSOD研究评估可扩展性，并构建ElasticNet验证骨干无关性。

Result: 在PANDA十亿像素基准上，ElasticFormer将骨干FLOPs降低80%，同时相比固定比例稀疏方法显著提升AP50。

Conclusion: 自适应、按前景比例分配的稀疏计算优于固定稀疏模式，且方法具备可扩展性与骨干无关性，可推广至弱监督目标检测及其他骨干结构。

Abstract: Recent advances in gigapixel-level imaging have brought High-Resolution Wide shots to the forefront of research. However, these images present significant challenges: extreme sparsity of foreground, gigapixel-level resolutions and diverse target counts. This makes traditional close-up detectors inaccurate and slow as they are overwhelmed by the background. Although previous research has explored sparse backbones, their fixed sparsity patterns lack the adaptability required to handle diverse target numbers. To address this, we introduce ElasticFormer, a sparse backbone that dynamically allocates computational resources based on foreground proportion. After scoring windows based on variance, proposed ElasticSelector module will predict the foreground proportion for top-k selection. The mechanism guides the model to select target-containing windows, scaling resources in areas where objects are clustered. We introduce a novel loss function combined with the 3-phase training strategy for ElasticSelector, allowing it to function properly when bounding box annotations are missing. A WSOD study is carried on PASCAL VOC 2007 to evaluate its extensibility. Further, ElasticNet is created to verify its backbone-agnostic nature. In experiments on the PANDA gigapixel benchmark, ElasticFormer reduces backbone FLOPs by 80% while achieving a significant improvement in AP50 when compared to fixed-ratio sparse methods.

</details>


### [147] [UniRA: Unified Representation Alignment and Auto-Encoding for Efficient Diffusion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39948)
*An Lanji,Dawei Liu,Jin Li,Haoran Xu,Mei Chen,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniRA将表征对齐(REPA)与表征自编码(RAE)统一到单一目标中，tokenizer输出同时作为扩散目标与对齐锚点，在ImageNet 256/512生成上FID匹配或超越SD3/REPA/RAE，训练步数最多减少5倍，并支持更强的零样本分类与线性探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT训练收敛慢、计算开销大。REPA通过中间特征对齐预训练表征模型（如DINOv2）加速收敛，RAE用自监督特征学习紧凑表征空间tokenizer替代像素VAE，但二者被孤立开发：REPA仍依赖像素VAE做隐扩散，RAE未利用对齐信号加速骨干。作者希望统一这两条互补路线。

Method: 提出UniRA统一框架，将表征对齐与表征自编码耦合进单一目标。学习一个表征空间tokenizer，其输出同时充当扩散目标与对齐锚点，使tokenizer、扩散骨干与外部表征模型三者联合优化。给出理论结果：该联合优化在保持外部模型表征质量的同时加速扩散收敛；并推导出平衡重建、对齐与生成三项目标的原则性加权方案。

Result: 在ImageNet类条件生成256×256与512×512上，UniRA的FID匹配或超越SOTA DiT（SD3、REPA、RAE），同时训练步数最多减少5倍。统一表征空间还带来更好的零样本分类与线性探测表现。

Conclusion: 生成与理解可从共享、对齐的隐空间共同受益；表征对齐与表征自编码并非独立技巧，而可在一个统一目标中相互增强。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for high-fidelity image synthesis, yet they suffer from slow training convergence and high computational cost. Two recent lines of work address this from complementary angles: Representation Alignment (REPA) accelerates DiT training by aligning intermediate features with a pretrained external representation model (e.g., DINOv2), while Representation Auto-Encoders (RAE) replace the pixel-space VAE with a compact representation-space tokenizer learned from self-supervised features. However, these approaches are developed and applied in isolation: REPA still relies on a pixel-space VAE for latent diffusion, and RAE does not exploit the alignment signal that accelerates the diffusion backbone. We present \textbf{UniRA}, a unified framework that couples representation alignment and representation auto-encoding into a single objective. UniRA learns a representation-space tokenizer whose output serves simultaneously as the diffusion target and the alignment anchor, so the tokenizer, the diffusion backbone, and the external representation model are co-optimized. We give a theoretical result showing that this joint optimization preserves the representation quality of the external model while accelerating diffusion convergence, and we derive a principled weighting that balances the reconstruction, alignment, and generation objectives. On ImageNet class-conditional generation at 256*256 and 512*512, UniRA matches or surpasses state-of-the-art DiTs (SD3, REPA, RAE) in FID while reducing training steps by up to 5*. We further show that the unified representation space supports improved zero-shot classification and linear probing, confirming that generation and understanding benefit from a shared, aligned latent space.

</details>


### [148] [Mind The Gap (MTG-AD): Dataset and Fine-grained Evaluation for End-to-End Inline Audio Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39955)
*Subhashini Venugopalan,Jenny Tan,Taylor Roper,Jimmy Tobin,Anton Kast,Alicia Martin,Sam Sepah,Amy Pavel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MTG-AD数据集（36小时UGC视频、8k+专业标注的内联音频描述）与MTG-AD-Eval无参考评估框架，端到端渲染音频以检验描述嵌入自然静默的能力，揭示前沿MLLM能准确描述视觉事件并大幅降低语音重叠，但在叙事节奏与流畅性上仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频描述（AD）对视障与低视力（BLV）用户获取视觉媒体至关重要。MLLM可支持按需生成AD，但其在"内联"AD（须嵌入视频既有静默间隙）上的表现，尤其是在多样化用户生成内容（UGC）上的表现，尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建MTG-AD：36小时视频（平均约5分钟），共8k+条描述，由盲人与明眼AD专业人员配对共同创作；2) 提出MTG-AD-Eval：基于AD专家指南、无需参考的细粒度可操作评估框架；3) 端到端评估：将生成脚本渲染为音轨，衡量其在原视频自然静默中的嵌入程度并最小化干扰性重叠；4) 与明眼评分者及7名BLV参与者的人工评分进行相关性验证。

Result: 前沿MLLM能准确描述视觉事件并显著减少语音重叠，但在叙事节奏与信息流动上仍存差距；自动化无参考指标与人工质量评分显著相关；用户略微更偏好人工撰写的AD，但认为MLLM生成的AD对理解内容高度有帮助。

Conclusion: 为内联AD提供高质量基准与可操作评估方法，给出系统设计建议，并公开MTG-AD数据集与MTG-AD-Eval框架。

Abstract: Audio descriptions (AD) are essential for making visual media accessible to blind and low-vision (BLV) users. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a scalable solution for generating audio descriptions on-demand, their performance on ``inline'' audio descriptions -- which must fit within existing silences in a video -- remains under-explored, particularly for diverse user-generated content (UGC). We present a comprehensive investigation into MLLM-generated inline audio descriptions. First, we introduce MTG-AD, a professionally annotated dataset of 36 hours of videos, each averaging $\sim$5 mins. and 8k+ descriptions in total, co-created by paired blind and sighted AD professionals to provide a high-quality benchmark for this task. Second, we propose MTG-AD-Eval, an evaluation framework based on audio description expert guidelines that provides fine-grained, reference-free actionable metrics for model improvement. Crucially, our framework evaluates the end-to-end task: it renders the generated scripts into audio tracks to assess their fit within the original video's natural silences, minimizing disruptive overlaps. Our analysis across frontier MLLMs reveals that while models accurately describe visual events and substantially reduce speech overlaps, gaps persist in narrative pacing and flow. Finally, we empirically validate that our automated and reference-free metrics correlate significantly with human quality ratings from sighted raters and 7 BLV participants, finding that while users narrowly prefer human-authored audio descriptions, they find MLLM-generated audio descriptions highly beneficial for comprehension. We provide actionable system design guidance and release MTG-AD at https://hf.co/datasets/google/mind-the-gap-inline-ad and MTG-AD-Eval at https://github.com/google/mtg-ad.

</details>


### [149] [DINOv2 for PET: A Pre-Training Domain Benchmark Across Classification, Segmentation, and Prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40018)
*Roxanne De Maeyer,Yannick Brackenier,Vicky De Ridder,Simon DeKeyser,Maarten Larmuseau,Jef Vandemeulebroucke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个在2D PET图像上预训练的DINOv2模型PET-DINOv2-2D，在分类、病灶分割和预后三项任务上匹配或超越自然图像DINOv2与OmniRad，且在少标签场景下优势最明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINO自监督预训练特征在放射影像（X-ray、CT、MRI）迁移良好，但文献指出其对PET等功能成像迁移较差；而PET专用DINO模型极少，且缺少跨任务、跨数据集的验证。

Method: 构建PET-DINOv2-2D：在2D PET数据上预训练DINOv2；与原始DINOv2（LVD-142M自然图像预训练）和OmniRad（CT/MRI/超声预训练）对比；所有骨干网络使用相同的轻量级下游头。

Result: 尽管预训练数据量远少于两个基线，PET-DINOv2-2D在正常/异常分类、病灶分割、无复发生存预后三项PET下游任务上均匹配或优于基线；优势在标签稀缺时最大。

Conclusion: PET域内自监督预训练可产生迁移性更强、标签效率更高的特征，PET专用基础模型具有明确价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: DINO (self-DIstillation with NO labels) has become a stan- dard for self-supervised pre-training, producing features that generalise across datasets and downstream tasks. In medical imaging it has been adopted mainly for radiology (X-ray, CT and MRI), where even natural- image DINO features transfer well. In the literature, these features have been reported to transfer worse to functional imaging such as PET. Yet PET-specific DINO models have barely been introduced, and even less validated across multiple tasks and datasets. We present PET-DINOv2-2D, the first DINOv2 model pre-trained on 2D PET, and benchmark it against open-source encoders: the origi- nal DINOv2 (pre-trained on the LVD-142M natural-image dataset) and OmniRad (pre-trained on CT, MRI and ultrasound). Using the same lightweight heads for every backbone, and despite far less pre-training data, PET-DINOv2-2D matches or outperforms both on three down- stream PET tasks: normal/abnormal classification, lesion segmentation and recurrence-free survival prognosis. Its advantage is largest when few labels are available, suggesting that PET pre-training gives more transferable, label-efficient features. Code is made available at https://github.com/nuclivision/pet-dinov2-2d/.

</details>


### [150] [Segment Anything Model Based Context Guided Mamba Unet for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40029)
*Chenchen Wang,You Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMBA-UNet：融合SAM上下文知识与Mamba层级特征的医学图像分割框架，通过HCEM注意力融合模块与LoRA/Adapter微调实现全局表征与局部细节的平衡，在Synapse、ISIC17、ISIC18上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割需兼顾全局上下文理解与细粒度结构精度。SAM泛化性强但缺乏医学领域适应性；基于SSM（如Mamba）的方法能高效建模长程依赖，却难以保留精细解剖细节。因此需融合二者优势。

Method: 提出SAMBA-UNet框架，整合SAM的上下文知识与Mamba的层级特征学习。核心为分层上下文增强模块（HCEM），基于注意力机制深度融合SAM多层级特征与Mamba特征以增强细节感知与分割精度；并采用LoRA与Adapter微调策略使SAM适配医学影像任务。

Result: 在Synapse、ISIC17、ISIC18等多个医学分割数据集上进行多指标综合评估，实验结果表明SAMBA-UNet达到state-of-the-art性能，在泛化性、分割精度与计算效率之间取得有效平衡。

Conclusion: 将SAM的通用上下文先验与Mamba的高效长程建模结合，并辅以参数高效微调，是提升医学图像分割性能的有效途径；代码将在论文录用后开源。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is crucial for clinical diagnosis and treatment planning, requiring both global contextual understanding and fine-grained structural precision. While the Segment Anything Model (SAM) demonstrates strong generalization capabilities, its direct application to medical images often lacks domain-specific adaptability. Meanwhile, State Space Models (SSMs)-based methods, such as Mamba, efficiently capture long-range dependencies but may struggle with preserving fine anatomical details. To address these challenges, we propose a segment anything model based context guided mamba unet, SAMBA-UNet, a novel framework that integrates SAM's context knowledge with Mamba's hierarchical feature learning to enhance both global representation and local detail refinement. Specifically, we introduce the Hierarchical Context Enhancement Module (HCEM), an attention-based fusion module that deeply incorporates the multi-level features of SAM and Mamba to improve detail perception and segmentation precision. Additionally, we employ LoRA and Adapter-based fine-tuning to adapt SAM to medical imaging tasks, further enhancing its domain-specific segmentation capability and robustness. We conducted a comprehensive study on several medical segmentation datasets, including Synapse, ISIC17, and ISIC18, evaluating our method across multiple metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that SAMBA-UNet achieves state-of-the-art performance, effectively balancing generalization, segmentation accuracy, and computational efficiency. Our code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [151] [EPC-RawDC: Equivariant Physics Constrained 3D Diffusion with Raw Data Consistency for CBCT to CT Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40046)
*Alzahra Altalib,Chunhui Li,Alessandro Perelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EPC-RawDC——条件3D潜扩散框架，将体素生成建模与源自同一扫描仪CBCT前向模型的两个互补物理约束结合，实现跨模拟与实测投影域的定量CBCT-to-CT合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT广泛用于图像引导与自适应放疗，但散射、噪声、重建伪影及不准确的HU值限制其定量应用；现有扩散类CBCT-to-CT合成方法基本是图像驱动的，对底层采集物理利用不足。

Method: 条件3D潜扩散框架，基于扫描仪特定的CBCT前向模型引入两项约束：(1) 几何等变投影约束，利用CBCT前向算子的旋转等变性，通过相应变换后的参考投影监督合成CT；(2) 原始数据一致性，将单次采集的含噪投影测量作为扫描特定监督用于训练。在JUST（Elekta实测临床投影）与SynthRAD2025（Varian几何+泊松统计模拟投影）两个独立队列上评估。

Result: 两队列SSIM均达0.97；PSNR较DDPM分别提升2.66 dB（SynthRAD2025）和2.10 dB（JUST）；MAE分别降低20.3%与19.0%；SynthRAD2025上投影一致性与等变误差分别降低55.0%与41.4%。

Conclusion: 将体素扩散先验与扫描仪几何及扫描特定投影测量相结合，可在模拟与实测投影域上改善定量CBCT-to-CT合成；代码将于论文发表后开源。

Abstract: Cone beam computed tomography (CBCT) is widely used for image guided and adaptive radiotherapy, but scatter, noise, reconstruction artifacts, and inaccurate Hounsfield units limit its quantitative use. Recent diffusion approaches for CBCT to CT synthesis remain largely image driven, with limited use of the underlying acquisition physics. We propose EPC-RawDC, a conditional 3D latent diffusion framework that combines volumetric generative modeling with two complementary constraints derived from the same scanner specific CBCT forward model. First, geometry equivariant projection constraint exploits the rotational equivariance of the CBCT forward operator by supervising the synthesized CT through correspondingly transformed reference projections. Second, raw data consistency uses noisy projection measurements of the individual acquisition as scan specific supervision during training. The framework was evaluated on two independent clinical cohorts with distinct scanner geometries and projection sources. For JUST, the physics constraints used measured clinical raw CBCT projections acquired with an Elekta system, whereas for SynthRAD2025 the projection data were simulated from the reference CT using a Varian geometry with Poisson photon statistics. EPC-RawDC achieved an SSIM of 0.97 in both cohorts and improved PSNR over DDPM by 2.66 dB on SynthRAD2025 and 2.10 dB on JUST, while reducing mean MAE by 20.3% and 19.0%, respectively. On SynthRAD2025, projection consistency and equivariant errors were reduced by 55.0% and 41.4% relative to DDPM. These results demonstrate that combining a volumetric diffusion prior with scanner geometry and scan specific projection measurements improves quantitative CBCT to CT synthesis across both simulated and measured projection domains. Code available after publication at https://github.com/ALZAHRAALTALIB/EPC-RawDC.

</details>


### [152] [Localizing Concepts in Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40061)
*Nanxiang Jiang,Yang Liu,Yuanhao Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的LoCo框架，在next-scale视觉自回归模型的Layer、Scale、Position三维度定位概念涌现位置与时序，并构建LoCoBench数据集；定位结果兼具可解释性与因果性，可高效支撑概念擦除、个性化与对抗概念注入等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的概念定位已有较多研究，但新兴的next-scale视觉自回归模型（VAR类）内部概念路由尚属空白；随着生成模型需要安全可控地适配，理解视觉概念在模型内部的路由机制成为关键需求。

Method: 提出LoCo，首个模型无关的概念定位框架，利用next-scale生成固有的由粗到细特性，将概念知识精确映射到Layer、Scale、Position三个维度；同时构建LoCoBench数据集（覆盖10类多样类别），以在无上下文偏置条件下系统定位与评估概念路由；并在Infinity、HunyuanImage-3.0、InfinityStar等图像与视频自回归模型上进行广泛探测。

Result: 探测表明定位到的位置既具有可解释性，又与概念涌现存在因果关系；基于该定位方法的定向干预在概念擦除、模型个性化、对抗概念注入三项应用上达到SOTA，显著降低计算开销并保持良性生成效用。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自回归生成过程中概念知识的内部路由规律，为更可解释、更高效、更安全的模型适配提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Understanding the internal routing of visual concepts is crucial for the safe and controllable adaptation of generative models. While concept localization has been widely studied in diffusion models, the emerging paradigm of next-scale visual autoregressive models remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce LoCo, the first model-agnostic framework to localize where and when specific concepts emerge within autoregressive models. Driven by the native coarse-to-fine nature of next-scale generation, our method precisely maps conceptual knowledge across three distinct dimensions: Layer, Scale, and Position. To systematically localize and evaluate concept routing without context bias, we propose LoCoBench, a comprehensive dataset spanning 10 diverse categories. Extensive probing on image and video autoregressive models like Infinity, HunyuanImage-3.0, and InfinityStar shows that the localized positions are both interpretable and causally related to concept emergence. Building on these insights, we apply our localization method to three key applications: concept erasure, model personalization, and adversarial concept injection. Experiments demonstrate that our targeted intervention achieves state-of-the-art performance, substantially reducing computational overhead while preserving benign utility. Overall, our findings offer insights into how conceptual knowledge is routed during autoregressive generation, introducing a practical pathway for more interpretable, efficient, and secure adaptation.

</details>


### [153] [BRACHIO: Broad Representations Across Centres for Head-and-neck Imaging Oncology (HECKTOR 2026)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40068)
*Roxanne De Maeyer,Ine Dirks,Yannick Brackenier,Simon DeKeyser,Jef Vandemeulebroucke,Vicky De Ridder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 团队 nucli 在 HECKTOR 2026 挑战赛中使用自监督预训练编码器（PET-DINOv2-MIPs）与多任务集成模型，完成头颈癌分割、TN 分期与无复发生存预测；分割 Dice 0.5409，T/N 分期平衡准确率 0.3962/0.6432，RFS C-index 0.6577（加权挑战分 0.5802）。预后任务表现接近上届最佳，但分割结果明显偏低并拖累分期任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HECKTOR 2026 挑战要求对多中心 PET/CT 完成原发灶与淋巴结勾画、TN 分期和 RFS 预后预测三类任务；这三类任务分别依赖稠密空间信息与全局信息，单一架构难以兼顾，故需探索自监督预训练编码器在多任务场景下的适配性，并检验以分割衍生特征驱动的分期与预后流程的可行性。

Method: (1) 在 HECKTOR 2026 PET 扫描上自监督预训练 2D DINOv2 编码器（PET-DINOv2-MIPs，基于 MIP 切片）；(2) 分割：集成从零训练的 DynUNet 与 Swin 主干由 SimMIM 预训练权重初始化的 SwinUNETR；(3) TN 分期：由分割掩膜提取 GTVp/GTVn 几何特征经逻辑回归（LR）得到分期概率，与 PET-DINOv2-MIPs 深度特征（PCA+LR）通过二级 LR 融合；(4) 预后：微调 PET-DINOv2-MIPs ViT-S 编码器最后两个 block 并联合 MLP 头，以 CoxPH 损失训练，将临床协变量与影像特征拼接。

Result: 分割集成模型平均 Dice 为 0.5409；TN 分期平衡准确率为 T 期 0.3962、N 期 0.6432；RFS 预后测试集 Harrell's C-index 为 0.6577，加权挑战总分为 0.5802。预后结果与上一届最佳成绩相当，且与内部交叉验证一致；分割结果显著低于该基准，并因此限制了依赖分割的分期表现。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练编码器结合特征级融合的多任务流程在预后任务上具备竞争力，但分割性能是整体流程的瓶颈，沿因果链传递并削弱 TN 分期效果；后续工作应优先提升分割精度（如更强预训练、3D 上下文或数据增强），以释放分期与整体挑战分数的上限。

Abstract: The HECKTOR 2026 challenge benchmarks machine learning for head and neck cancer, where teams delineate primary and nodal tumour volumes, perform TN staging, and predict recurrence-free survival (RFS). This report describes the submissions for the HECKTOR challenge of team nucli. As the three tasks span both dense and global information from multicentric PET/CT scans, we utilise self-supervised pre-trained encoders, training a 2D DINOv2 encoder (PET-DINOv2-MIPs) on HECKTOR 2026's PET scans. Our final test submission ensembles a DynUNet trained from scratch with a SwinUNETR whose Swin trunk was initialised from a SimMIM-pre-trained checkpoint for segmentation, reaching a mean Dice of 0.5409. For TN staging, GTVp/GTVn geometric features from the segmentation masks are fit to stage probabilities by logistic regression (LR) and fused with PET-DINOv2-MIPs deep features (PCA + LR) via a second-level LR, reaching balanced accuracies of 0.3962 (T-stage) and 0.6432 (N-stage). For the RFS prognosis task, we fine-tune the last two blocks of the PET-DINOv2-MIPs ViT-S encoder jointly with an MLP head under a CoxPH loss, concatenating clinical covariates with the imaging features, reaching a test Harrell's C-index of 0.6577 and a weighted challenge score of 0.5802. The prognosis result is comparable to the best entry of the previous edition and consistent with our internal cross-validation, while our segmentation result is well below it and limits the staging results that depend on it.

</details>


### [154] [Uncertainty-aware Coarse-to-Fine Main Pancreatic Duct Segmentation for Width Measurement in 3D MRCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40073)
*Zipei Zhang,Jaakko Sahlsten,Mikko Saukkoriipi,Johanna Ronkainen,Mona Lehto,Akseli Aittoniemi,Irina Rinta-Kiikka,Kimmo Kaski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的深度学习流水线，从容积MRCP扫描中自动分割主胰管并进行最大宽度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主胰管（MPD）评估对胰管扩张诊断与胰腺癌分析至关重要，但人工判读存在读者间差异；而基于容积MRCP的全自动、不确定性感知的MPD分析研究仍较薄弱。

Method: 采用两阶段级联框架：粗定位模型先确定胰管感兴趣区域，精细模型在该区域内分割MPD；数据为69例患者共256份MPD已标注MRCP扫描，留出14例患者、55个体积作测试；使用五模型交叉验证集成提升性能并估计不确定性。

Result: 最佳分割性能为DSC 0.634、精确率0.744、召回率0.593；最大MPD宽度预测平均绝对误差0.355 mm；扩张MPD（≥5 mm）检测灵敏度75.0%、特异度74.4%。

Conclusion: 结果表明自动化MPD分割可辅助基于MRCP的胰管测量，为该方向提供了新的基线结果。

Abstract: Assessment of the main pancreatic duct (MPD) from volumetric magnetic resonance cholangiopancreatography (MRCP) scans is an essential task for ductal dilatation diagnosis and pancreatic cancer analysis. However, manual assessment is affected by reader variability in which automated analysis may assist. Nevertheless, fully automated and uncertainty-aware MPD analysis from volumetric MRCP remains underexplored area of research. In this study, we propose an automatic and uncertainty-aware deep learning-based pipeline for MPD segmentation and maximum-width analysis from MRCP scans. A coarse localisation model first identifies the ductal region-of-interest, followed by a fine model that segments the MPD within the region. The dataset consists of 256 MPD-segmented MRCP scans from 69 patients. A randomly selected holdout test subset of 14 patients with 55 MRCP volumes was used for evaluation. We employed a five-model cross-validation ensemble for improved performance and uncertainty estimation. The best MPD segmentation performance was 0.634 DSC, 0.744 precision, and 0.593 recall. The mean absolute error for maximum MPD width prediction was 0.355~mm and dilated MPD ($\geq 5$~mm) was detected with 75.0\% sensitivity and 74.4\% specificity. These novel results suggest that automated MPD segmentation can be used to aid MRCP-based duct measurements.

</details>


### [155] [Morphology-Aware Channel-Decoupled Mamba Network for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40085)
*Chenchen Wang,You Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出形态感知通道解耦Mamba网络（MCDNet），在Synapse腹部CT上实现81.61%平均Dice的多器官分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT多器官分割受器官形状尺寸差异大、组织对比度低与边界模糊影响，异构解剖结构建模困难。

Method: 核心为形态感知通道解耦状态空间（MCSS）块：将特征通道分组处理；组内用形态感知增强模块（MAEM）结合可变形卷积与通道注意力，使局部特征提取适应形状变化；再做组内状态空间长程建模，并以通道门控自适应融合。

Result: 在Synapse腹部CT数据集上平均Dice达81.61%，验证模型有效性。

Conclusion: 将形态自适应的局部增强与通道解耦的长程建模相结合，可有效应对器官形态多变与边界模糊问题，提升多器官分割精度。

Abstract: Accurate multi-organ segmentation in computed tomography (CT) is essential for computer-aided diagnosis, treatment planning, and disease monitoring. However, substantial variations in organ shape and size, together with low tissue contrast and ambiguous boundaries, complicate the modeling of heterogeneous anatomical structures. To address these challenges, we propose a Morphology-Aware Channel-Decoupled Mamba Network (MCDNet), which integrates adaptive local structural enhancement with channel-decoupled long-range modeling. Specifically, we introduce a Morphology-Aware Channel-Decoupled State-Space (MCSS) block that divides feature channels into separate processing groups, allowing differentiated structural representations to be learned within an efficient state-space framework. Within each group, a Morphology-Aware Enhancement Module (MAEM) combines deformable convolutions with channel attention to adapt local feature extraction to anatomical shape variations. Group-wise state-space modeling then captures long-range spatial dependencies, and channel gating adaptively integrates the resulting features. Experiments on the Synapse abdominal CT dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of MCDNet, achieving an average Dice Similarity Coefficient of 81.61% for multi-organ segmentation.

</details>


### [156] [Neural Blind Deconvolution for Image Scanning Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40088)
*Eliot Guez,Nathan Buskulic,Lisa Cuneo,Luca Calatroni,Giuseppe Vicidomini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练盲DIP框架，联合估计样本与探测器相关PSF，实现ISM超分辨重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISM通过逐探测器元素扫描图像的多图像反卷积实现超分辨，但其精度依赖PSF模型，而样本与系统像差常使理论PSF失效。

Method: 利用扫描图像间差异隐含像差信息，构建免训练的盲DIP框架联合估计样本与探测器相关PSF；PSF由探测器几何与共享激发/发射PSF参数化，减少未知量并保持物理ISM模型；采用高斯预初始化稳定盲优化。

Result: 在实验微管管蛋白数据上，盲DIP恢复出比多图像反卷积及固定理论PSF的DIP更精细的纤维结构，且PSF能自适应测量数据。

Conclusion: 盲DIP可在无需训练与精确PSF先验的情况下提升ISM重建质量，具备实际成像场景的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Image scanning microscopy (ISM) acquires one scanned image per detector element, which are then fused into a single image by multi-image deconvolution to obtain a super-resolved image. Its accuracy, however, depends on point spread function (PSF) models that sample- and system-induced aberrations often invalidate. Since differences between the scanned images encode aberration information, we propose a training-free blind Deep Image Prior (DIP) framework that jointly estimates the specimen and the detector-dependent PSFs. The PSFs are parametrised through the detector geometry and shared excitation and emission PSFs, reducing the unknowns while preserving the physical ISM model, and a Gaussian pre-initialisation stabilises the blind optimisation. On experimental tubulin data, blind DIP recovers finer filamentous structures than multi-image deconvolution and DIP with fixed theoretical PSFs, while adapting the PSFs to the measurements.

</details>


### [157] [RepConTUR: A Report-Conditioned Volumetric Multimodal Model for Abdominal Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40094)
*Seunghwan Lee,Sangjoon Park,Jongmin Lee,Yoon Jo Kim,Wonyoung Cho,Jin Sung Kim,Han Joo Chae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RepConTUR 是一个原生3D、早期融合的多模态模型，以影像报告为条件进行腹部肿瘤体积分割，并联合训练RES、GSEG、RRG、VQA四项任务，在域内外多个指标上优于文本提示与纯视觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗计划需要肿瘤勾画，但自动分割存在大量假阳性误差，扭曲剂量分布并增加人工编辑时间。影像报告随扫描提供，可在亚器官层面定位病灶，然而现有可文本提示模型仅以短指代短语为条件，且多为2D架构，难以利用叙述性报告与体积信息。

Method: 将 AbdomenAtlas 3.0 扩展为亚器官级可提示分割数据集，并生成基于报告的VQA对，在同一标注上构建四项报告衍生任务（RES、GSEG、RRG、VQA）。模型由3D CT编码器、早期融合语言模型和原生3D分割模块构成，四项任务联合训练。

Result: 在肝、肾、胰腺上，Dice、实例级精确率与检测F1均优于可文本提示基线；加入报告后平均精确率提升而召回相当。对比 nnU-Net 与 R-Super，在肝、胰腺的域内全部指标最优，并在四个分布外公开队列上取得最高平均DSC、召回、精确率与检测F1。消融显示：报告条件将检测F1由0.499提升至0.528；联合GSEG训练将RES F1由0.450提升至0.482；原生3D分割模块将GSEG F1由2D的0.139提升至0.528。

Conclusion: 以叙述性报告为条件、原生3D早期融合的多任务联合训练可有效抑制假阳性、提升检测与分割质量，为报告引导的放疗靶区勾画提供可行范式。

Abstract: Tumor delineation is required for radiotherapy planning, yet automatic segmentation is limited by errors, predominantly false positives, that distort dose and increase editing time. The radiology report accompanies the scan and localizes disease at the suborgan level, but existing text-promptable models condition on short referring phrases, and most operate in 2D. We present RepConTUR, a single native-3D early-fusion multimodal model that conditions volumetric abdominal tumor segmentation on the narrative report. We extend AbdomenAtlas 3.0 to a suborgan-level promptable segmentation dataset and generate report-based VQA pairs, yielding four report-derived tasks on the same annotations: referring segmentation (RES), report-grounded segmentation (GSEG), report generation (RRG), and visual question answering (VQA). We train RepConTUR, comprising a 3D CT encoder, an early-fusion language model, and a native-3D segmentation module, jointly on the four tasks. Across liver, kidney and pancreas, RepConTUR attains the highest Dice similarity coefficient (DSC), instance-wise precision, and detection F1 among the text-promptable baselines, and adding the report raises mean precision while leaving recall comparable. Against two vision-only specialists, nnU-Net and the report-supervised R-Super, RepConTUR attains the highest value on every in-domain metric for liver and pancreas and the highest mean DSC, recall, precision, and detection F1 over the four out-of-distribution public cohorts. Ablations indicate that conditioning on the narrative report raises detection F1 from 0.499 to 0.528 within the same model, that joint GSEG training raises RES F1 from 0.450 to 0.482, and that the native-3D segmentation module raises GSEG F1 from 0.139 to 0.528 over its 2D counterpart. Code is available at https://github.com/Oncosoft-Research/RepConTUR.

</details>


### [158] [SelfThinker: Self-Evolving Reasoning via Reliability-Verified Intrinsic Visual Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40115)
*Zhangquan Chen,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SelfThinker：首个自进化框架，将统一多模态模型自生成的"视觉思维"转化为更强的理解能力，无需人工图像标注，且推理时不生成中间图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型兼具生成与理解能力，可用图像合成作为视觉理解的训练监督。但现有视觉思维链方法依赖人工标注的辅助图像，且测试时需缓慢的图像生成，成本高且难以泛化。

Method: 给定视觉问题，模型从生成通路采样候选辅助图像；用冻结的理解通路以条件点互信息（conditional PMI）为每个候选打分，筛选高效用视觉思维；高效用思维一方面通过流匹配（flow matching）微调生成器，另一方面作为特权教师上下文用于on-policy自蒸馏。推理时仅从原图直接文本推理，不生成中间图像。

Result: 仅在小规模合成的视觉搜索与导航数据上训练，却在自然图像与空间基准上广泛迁移：ThinkMorph-7B 的七基准平均从 52.7 提升至 64.0（+11.3）；在受控图像扰动探测中，相比 ThinkMorph-7B 最高提升 40.4 分，相比预训练骨干 BAGEL-7B 最高提升 23.2 分，涵盖细粒度与空间感知任务。

Conclusion: 验证了"自生成视觉思维作为自监督信号"的可行性：无需人工图像标注、无需测试时图像生成，即可通过生成—理解通路的自进化闭环显著增强统一模型的多模态理解与空间感知能力，并具备良好的跨任务迁移性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models can both generate and understand images within a single set of weights, offering an opportunity to use image synthesis as training supervision for visual comprehension. However, existing visual chain-of-thought methods rely on human-annotated helper images during training and require slow image generation at test time. We present SelfThinker, the first self-evolving framework that turns a unified model's self-generated visual thoughts into stronger understanding without human image annotations. Given a visual problem, SelfThinker samples candidate helper images from its generation pathway and uses its frozen understanding pathway to score each candidate via conditional pointwise mutual information. High-utility visual thoughts then refine the generator via flow matching and serve as privileged teacher context for on-policy self-distillation. At inference, SelfThinker reasons directly from the original image in text without generating intermediate images. Although trained only on small synthetic visual-search and navigation datasets, SelfThinker transfers broadly to natural-image and spatial benchmarks, raising the seven-benchmark average of ThinkMorph-7B from 52.7 to 64.0 (+11.3 points). Controlled image-perturbation probes further show that SelfThinker surpasses ThinkMorph-7B by up to 40.4 points and its pretrained BAGEL-7B backbone by up to 23.2 points across fine-grained and spatial perception tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangquanchen/SelfThinker.

</details>


### [159] [SynthFaces: Balanced Large Scale Human Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40119)
*Sergey Karpukhin,Denis Bobkov,Kirill Morozov,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用流匹配生成模型在身份嵌入流形上扩展Arc2Face，实现可控合成多样化人脸数据集SynthFaces，兼顾生成保真度与身份/标签多样性，并降低对真实个人图像的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人类中心数据集存在身份表征不均衡与敏感隐私信息，导致生成模型性能不公平，尤其在身份保持生成任务中；因此需要可替代的合成、均衡、隐私友好的训练数据。

Method: 构建可控生成流水线：以Arc2Face扩散模型为基础，叠加一个在身份嵌入流形上运行的flow matching（流匹配）生成模型，用于采样并生成多样身份的人脸；据此收集并情境化构建身份均衡数据集SynthFaces，并以最近邻非泄漏指标检验身份可分性。

Result: 方法同时实现生成保真度与标签多样性，其均衡程度在真实人类中心数据集中不具备；发布的图像与身份均为合成，经验证身份间可分离且无明显身份泄漏；在个性化生成的adapter方法微调中展示可用性。

Conclusion: SynthFaces可作为面向多下游任务的公平模型训练与微调数据，减少对真实个人图像的依赖并缓解偏差；其可扩展为可控数据合成范式。

Abstract: The pursuit of fair and equitable AI in human-centric tasks is critically hindered by the inherent biases and privacy risks of real-world datasets, which offer imbalanced representations of identity and often contain sensitive personal information. Consequently, state-of-the-art generative models trained on this skewed data exhibit unfair performance, particularly in identity-preserving generation. In this work we propose a pipeline for controllable generation of dataset with diverse faces for generative models training and finetuning. We achieve this by expanding foundational Arc2Face diffusion model with a flow matching generative model in the manifold of identity embeddings. We demonstrate that such approach achieves both goals of generative fidelity and label diversity not present in real human-centric datasets. As a result we collect and further contextualize a highly balanced identity-centric dataset of humans named SynthFaces. By design, our released images and identities are synthetic, reducing direct reliance on real personal images; we empirically check identity separation with a nearest-neighbor non-leakage metric. We purpose it for training and tuning of fair models for various downstream tasks, and showcase its usability in finetuning an existing adapter-based method for personalized generation.

</details>


### [160] [Learning a Motion Manifold to Constrain Ambiguity in Unsupervised 4D Medical Image Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40121)
*Haojin Li,Huazhu Fu,Heng Li,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出学习运动流形的无监督4D医学图像插值框架，通过跨样本谱基学习与样本条件Cayley基重定向限制运动歧义，在ACDC和4D-Lung上取得无中间监督方法中的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间稀疏观测下的无监督4D插值存在运动歧义：不同运动场可达成相当的端点一致性，却产生不同的中间解剖结构。当需要从仅有的端点估计大量独立可调的运动变量时，该歧义难以被约束。

Method: 学习运动流形以限制候选运动场同时保留样本特异变化。两部分组成：(1) 跨样本谱基学习（Cross-Sample Spectral Basis Learning），从端点导出的稠密估计中提取重复出现的运动结构，编码为共享谱基；(2) 样本条件Cayley基重定向（Sample-Conditioned Cayley Basis Reorientation），用预测的运动坐标旋转每个端点对的有效运动方向而不增加独立坐标数。二者复合构成跨端点对的完整速度场非线性族，积分后支持任意中间时刻的双向重建。

Result: 理论分析给出端点观测可区分所学运动族内邻近运动的充分条件；在ACDC与4D-Lung上，相较于所评估的无中间体数据训练方法达到SOTA，并与使用中间监督的方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 以低维、可重定向的共享谱基参数化运动场，可在不增加独立自由度的前提下缓解无监督4D插值的运动歧义，兼顾表示约束与样本适应性。

Abstract: Unsupervised 4D medical image interpolation reconstructs intermediate anatomical states from temporally sparse observations without intermediate supervision. Distinct motion fields may attain comparable endpoint agreement while producing different intermediate anatomy, giving rise to motion ambiguity. This ambiguity is difficult to constrain when many independently adjustable motion variables must be estimated from the observed endpoints. We develop a framework that learns a motion manifold to restrict candidate motion fields while retaining sample-specific variation. Cross-Sample Spectral Basis Learning extracts recurring motion structure from endpoint-derived dense estimates and encodes it in a shared spectral basis. Sample-Conditioned Cayley Basis Reorientation uses predicted motion coordinates to rotate the effective motion directions for each endpoint pair without increasing the number of independent coordinates. Their composition defines a nonlinear family of complete velocity fields across endpoint pairs, accommodating anatomical and motion-state differences within a restricted representation. Integrating each predicted field supports bidirectional reconstruction at arbitrary intermediate times. Theoretical analysis establishes sufficient conditions under which endpoint observations distinguish nearby motions within the learned family. Experiments on ACDC and 4D-Lung demonstrate state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods trained without intermediate volumes and competitive results against methods using intermediate supervision.

</details>


### [161] [It Takes Two to Probe, Thousands to Retrieve: Putting Vision--Language Probes to the Retrieval Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40124)
*Ivana Beňová,Michal Gregor,Marián Šimko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文比较了图像-文本匹配探针（SVO、SugarCrepe）与检索评估两种评价方式的关系：探针负例来源（基准手工负例、随机负例、其他模型检索负例）显著影响难度，且基准标签不完整导致SVO检索分数被低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型常用两种方式评测细粒度理解：图像-文本匹配探针（正确项只需胜过手工负例）与检索（正确项需胜过数千候选项）。二者关系不明，且检索分数普遍偏低，需要厘清探针难度与检索表现之间的关联及其成因。

Method: 将SVO与SugarCrepe两个探针基准转化为检索任务：对100个图像/文本查询，用人工标注者和LLM评估器（o3）判定六个VLM在全量数据上的top-10检索结果；在保持查询不变的前提下，仅改变负例来源（基准负例、随机抽取、其他模型的判定错误检索结果），比较模型对候选池的排序表现。

Result: SVO上，正确项胜过手工负例的准确率（85%）显著高于胜过检索负例（77%）；10个检索负例构成的候选池即可达到200–430个随机负例的区分效果。SugarCrepe上趋势相反，其手工负例比检索负例更难。SVO的低检索分数主要源于相关性标签不完整：人工标签下top-1正确率达54–90%，而基准标签下仅6–21%。未发现"通过探针"与"检索到正确项"之间存在关联。LLM评估器o3与单个标注者的一致性（κ 0.57–0.61）略低于标注者之间的一致性（0.64–0.68）。

Conclusion: 探针与检索评估衡量的是不同难度构念，基准手工负例的难度因数据集而异；检索指标偏低很大程度由标签不完整造成，而非模型能力不足。说明应谨慎用探针通过率推断检索能力，并需完善相关性标注。

Abstract: Vision--language models (VLMs) are tested for fine-grained understanding with image--text matching probes, a model must prefer the correct image or caption over handcrafted negative. In retrieval, the correct item must beat thousands of alternatives. We measure how these two evaluation methods relate by using two probing benchmarks, \svo{} and SugarCrepe, for retrieval tasks. For 100 image and caption queries we judge the top-10 retrievals from full dataset of six VLMs, with human annotators and an LLM assessor. We than compare how well models rank pool of items on the same queries, while changing only where the negatives comes from: the benchmark, a random draw, or the judged-incorrect retrievals of other models. On \svo{} the accuracy of retrieving a positive over handcrafted negatives is significantly higher than for retrieved ones (the correct item is preferred in 85\% against 77\% of the time). Using pool of ten retrieved negatives leads to same accuracy as when one uses pool of 200 to 430 random negatives. On SugarCrepe the order is reversed: its handcrafted negatives are harder than the retrieved ones. Incomplete relevance labels explain most of the low retrieval scores on \svo{}, where the top-ranked item is correct for 54--90\% of the queries under human labels against 6--21\% under the benchmark's labels. However, we detect no association between passing the probe for a query and retrieving a correct item first. The LLM assessor o3 agrees with individual annotators ($κ$ 0.57--0.61) slightly less than they agree with each other (0.64--0.68).

</details>


### [162] [VideoMemBench: How Well Do MLLMs Remember in Video Conversations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40131)
*Zhengdong Hu,Chao Wang,Kaituo Feng,Huanjin Yao,Mingfei Lu,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VideoMemBench，用带类型化跨轮依赖的长多轮对话评测 MLLM 的"上下文视频记忆"，最佳模型 Claude Opus 5 仅得 45.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 已能处理小时级视频，单视频长多轮对话成为自然使用模式，要求在对话中检索并复用"看过"与"说过"的信息（in-context video memory）；但现有视频 QA 基准的题目彼此独立、无需跨轮携带信息，无法度量该能力。

Method: 构建 VideoMemBench：每个样本为一个视频配一段长多轮对话，轮次由类型化跨轮依赖连接——Episodic Dependency（回指此前轮次描述的视觉片段，测"看过"记忆）与 Answer Dependency（基于模型自身早前回答，测"说过"记忆）；每条依赖需满足必要性（无早期轮次则不可答）与非泄漏性（不复述早期信息）；采用 planner、generator、judger 三角色 LLM 流水线生成并由人类专家审核。

Result: 数据集含 326 个视频、2,476 轮对话，其中 1,387 轮依赖早期轮次，包含多父依赖与长程回指；在 20+ 个开源与闭源 MLLM 上评测，最佳 Claude Opus 5 总体得分仅 45.7%。

Conclusion: 现有 MLLM 的上下文视频记忆能力显著不足；作者发布数据与评测，为该能力提供专门基准。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can now process hour-long videos, making extended multi-turn dialogue over a single video a natural mode of use. Sustaining such a dialogue requires more than answering each question in isolation: the model must retrieve and reuse information established earlier in the conversation, both what it has seen and what it has said, an ability we term in-context video memory. Existing video QA benchmarks leave this ability unmeasured: their questions are mutually independent, never reference one another, and each is answerable from the video alone, so nothing ever requires carrying information across turns. We introduce VideoMemBench, in which each sample pairs a single video with a long multi-turn dialogue, whose turns are connected by typed cross-turn dependencies: an Episodic Dependency refers back to a visual episode described in an earlier turn, probing memory of what the model has seen, while an Answer Dependency builds on the model's own earlier answer, probing memory of what the model has said. Every dependency is verified considering two properties: 1) necessity, meaning the question cannot be answered without its earlier turns, and 2) non-leakage, meaning the question does not restate the information from its earlier turns. To enforce these properties, we design a construction pipeline with three LLM roles: a planner, a generator, and a judger, with each dialogue reviewed by human experts. VideoMemBench comprises 326 videos with 2,476 turns, of which 1,387 depend on earlier turns, including multi-parent dependencies and long-range call-back. Among over 20 evaluated open- and closed-source MLLMs, Claude Opus 5 performs the best with an overall score of only 45.7\%, highlighting the challenge of in-context video memory. Our dataset and evaluation are released.

</details>


### [163] [NeurEO: dissecting Earth observation embeddings with computational neuroscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40137)
*Thijs L van der Plas,Jacob JW Bakermans,Ioannis N Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文借鉴计算神经科学方法（把AlphaEarth嵌入特征分别视为“自发神经活动”和“刺激-响应神经活动”）来解读地球嵌入的语义，发现其具有相关性与多尺度结构，对土地覆盖和高程存在高度特异且具方向性的调谐，并在去除主要趋势后呈现出更细粒度、有效维度更高的表征细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球嵌入（Earth embeddings）虽能提升下游地理空间任务性能，但语义含义未知、可解释性下降，制约可信应用的开发，因此需要为地球嵌入赋予可解释的语义。

Method: 迁移计算神经科学中解读生物神经元活动的方法：将嵌入特征作为独立数据分析（类比自发神经活动），并分析与土地覆盖、高程等协变量的关系（类比刺激-响应神经活动），进行相关结构、多尺度结构与调谐分析。

Result: 嵌入特征表现出相关性和多尺度结构；可对土地覆盖与高程呈现高度特异、具方向性的调谐（如“南/北向林地边缘”响应）；去除土地覆盖响应的主要趋势（如水-陆调谐）后，发现更细粒度的表征细节及更高的有效维度。

Conclusion: 该分析初步揭示了地球嵌入所表征的内容，为构建可信的下游应用提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Earth embeddings are compressed representations of rich Earth observation data that enable downstream models to tackle a wide range of complex geospatial tasks. Yet, this increase in performance comes at the cost of decreased interpretability, as the semantic meaning of Earth embeddings is unknown. To address this challenge, we borrow methods from the field of computational neuroscience, which aim to interpret activity in (biological) neurons. Here, we adapt and apply these methods to assign meaning to AlphaEarth embeddings. We analyse embedding features both as stand-alone data, equivalent to spontaneous neural activity, and in relation to land cover and elevation correlates, equivalent to stimulus-response neural activity. We demonstrate correlational and multi-scale structure in the embedding features. Embedding features can display highly specific and directional tuning to land cover and elevation, such as a `North vs South woodland edge' response. When we remove the main trends of combined land cover responses (like water versus land tuning), we discover fine-grained representational details with increased effective dimensionality. Together, our analyses start to unravel what these Earth embeddings represent, which is critical for developing trustworthy downstream applications.

</details>


### [164] [Hierarchical Step Decomposition for Training-free Online Video Step Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40153)
*Long Zhou,Luca Zanella,Paolo Rota,Massimiliano Mancini,Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的层级步骤分解框架HSD，用于在线视频步骤定位（VSG），在HT-Step、Ego4D Goal-Step、CrossTask上显著优于SOTA，识别更准、定位更精、推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VSG方法要么离线（需完整视频）、要么在线但依赖固定长度片段，导致输出噪声大、过度分割；同时在线场景下难以从流式视频构建语义连贯的时间片段，且粗粒度步骤描述与视觉观测之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: 基于大型多模态模型（LMM）的免训练框架：1）增量地将流入片段聚合成语义一致的事件，用持续与视觉流校验的锚点描述（anchor caption）保证连贯性；2）在事件层面做零样本步骤分类；3）对不确定预测选择性细化，将候选步骤分解为细粒度子步骤以捕捉更细视觉证据。

Result: 在HT-Step、Ego4D Goal-Step和CrossTask三个基准上大幅超越现有SOTA，实现更好的步骤识别、更准确的时间定位与更快的推理速度；因在语义事件而非每个固定片段上操作，减少了冗余计算并提升预测稳定性。

Conclusion: HSD通过层级化的语义事件构建与子步骤细化，有效解决在线VSG的片段连贯性与语义鸿沟问题，且无需训练即可取得更优且更高效的在线步骤定位性能。

Abstract: Given a set of procedural steps, Video Step Grounding (VSG) aims to identify which steps occur in a video and localize when they are performed. Existing methods are either offline, requiring access to the full video before making predictions, or online, but relying on fixed-length video segments producing noisy, over-segmented outputs. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Step Decomposition (HSD), a training-free framework for online VSG that leverages the semantic understanding capabilities of Large Multimodal Models. HSD addresses two central challenges in online grounding: constructing semantically coherent temporal segments from a streaming video, and bridging the semantic gap between coarse procedural step descriptions and visual observations. To construct elementary temporal units, HSD incrementally groups incoming segments into semantically consistent events, using an anchor caption that is continuously validated against the visual stream. Based on these events, we perform zero-shot procedural step classification and selectively refine uncertain predictions by decomposing candidate steps into finegrained substeps that capture more nuanced visual evidence. By operating on semantically coherent events rather than every fixed-length segment, HSD reduces redundant computation and produces more stable predictions. Experiments on HT-Step, Ego4D Goal-Step, and CrossTask show that HSD substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods, overall achieving better step recognition, more accurate temporal localization, and faster inference.

</details>


### [165] [EventSeeColor: Learning to Reconstruct Color from Monochrome Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40166)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Langyi Chen,Haoxian Zhou,Zelin Zhang,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Xiaoming Chen,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EventSeeColor，用事件重建的灰度图与对齐事件共同引导扩散着色器，从单色事件相机实现彩色图像重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事件图像重建只恢复灰度强度，无法表达同亮度不同表面间的外观差异；而事件触发模式依赖表面反射率、光照与传感器光谱响应，可能隐含可用于颜色重建的线索。

Method: 以事件重建灰度图与对齐事件共同作为条件引导扩散着色模型；灰度提供语义上下文以支持颜色推断，并引入区域均衡的色度监督与局部RGB细化以改善色彩分布、亮度与纹理。

Result: 在两个真实事件数据集联合评估中，EventSeeColor在外部对比方法中取得最佳结构相似度、学习感知指标及色度区域色差。

Conclusion: 单色事件相机的触发模式可支撑颜色重建，事件+灰度联合引导的扩散着色方案在结构与感知色彩质量上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Event cameras capture visual brightness changes under challenging lighting conditions. However, traditional event-based image reconstruction targets grayscale intensity and produces images without scene color. Color distinguishes surfaces with similar intensity. Reconstructing color from events can reveal appearance differences that reconstructed grayscale images cannot express. Although monochrome event cameras do not directly measure color, their event-triggering patterns depend on surface reflectance, illumination, and the sensor's spectral response. These patterns may contain cues that support color reconstruction. In this paper, we present EventSeeColor, which uses event-reconstructed grayscale and aligned events to jointly guide a diffusion colorizer. Grayscale-derived semantic context supports color inference, while region-balanced chroma supervision and local RGB refinement improve color distribution, brightness, and texture. In the combined evaluation on two real event datasets, EventSeeColor achieves the best structural similarity, learned perceptual scores, and chromatic-region color difference among the external methods.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond CCT: Multi-channel SPD recovery for per-shot camera color correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40175)
*Shuwei Yue,Hongke Wei,Qiao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用多通道环境光传感器+白点约束，通过NNLS从基字典中恢复光源SPD，逐帧闭式拟合3x3 CCM，改善LED等非普朗克光源的色彩校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机用2~3个预标定3x3矩阵按CCT插值做色彩校正，把光源SPD压缩成一个标量；LED、荧光灯与混合光源偏离普朗克轨迹，同色度异谱光源需要不同最优CCM，CCT流程无法区分。

Method: 以相机白点和多通道环境光传感器（ALS）为条件，对基字典做非负最小二乘（NNLS）恢复光源SPD，再逐张照片闭式求解3x3 CCM。

Result: 在300个光源、3100个反射率的合成基准上，平均角度误差由1.89°降至1.20°；LED类非普朗克组（n=45）尾部误差降低1.7倍。该增益在ALS不完美与观测噪声下仍成立，并在1684条实测SPD上保持非普朗克优势。

Conclusion: 用光谱级光源估计替代CCT标量插值，可有效缓解异谱同色度歧义，提升非普朗克及混合光照下的色彩校正精度。

Abstract: Modern cameras correct color via two or three pre-calibrated 3x3 matrices interpolated by Correlated Color Temperature (CCT). This collapses the illuminant SPD to a scalar. LEDs, fluorescents, and mixed sources sit off the Planckian locus, and metameric illuminants with the same chromaticity demand different optimal CCMs that a CCT pipeline cannot distinguish. We recover the illuminant SPD instead using non-negative least squares against a basis dictionary, conditioned on the camera white point and a multi-channel ambient-light sensor (ALS). A closed-form 3x3 CCM is then fit per shot. On a synthetic benchmark of 300 illuminants and 3,100 reflectances, mean angular error drops from 1.89 to 1.20 degrees, with a 1.7x tail reduction on the LED-like off-Planckian group (n=45) where metameric ambiguity is tangible. The gain holds under realistic ALS imperfections and observation noise, and the same off-Planckian advantage carries over to 1,684 real measured SPDs.

</details>


### [167] [AdMuse: Skill-Guided Creative Advertising Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40190)
*Minyan Luo,Yuxin Zhang,Meng Zhao,Xincan Wang,Fuzhang Wu,Tong-Yee Lee,Oliver Deussen,Fan Tang,Weiming Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdMuse是一个技能引导的多智能体框架，将高质量广告抽象为可复用的创意技能，用于跨领域的广告策略生成与图像创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意广告图像既需吸引注意力，又要传达产品定位。在渲染前选择合适的"概念桥梁"（conceptual bridge）是广告生成中的难点，现有方法难以有效解决。

Method: 不检索孤立样例，而是将反复出现的桥梁模式抽象为技能库以指导新产品简报。生成时协同三部分：技能路由（skill routing）、多候选构思与选择（相对判断选出最优方向）、VLM引导的视觉精修。将广告创意视为结构化决策过程而非单次提示写作步骤。

Result: 在多样化产品与品牌定位上的实验表明，AdMuse能生成视觉上具有吸引力且忠实传达产品定位的广告。

Conclusion: 以技能抽象与多智能体协同的结构化决策流程替代单步提示生成，可有效提升广告创意在注意力吸引力与定位传达两方面的表现。

Abstract: An outstanding creative advertising image should both attract attention and communicate a product's positioning. How to choose a persuasive conceptual bridge before rendering begins is challenging for advertising generation. We present AdMuse, a skill-guided multi-agent framework that distills high-quality advertisements into reusable creative skills for cross-domain advertising strategy. Rather than retrieving isolated exemplars, AdMuse abstracts recurring bridge patterns into a skill library that can guide new product briefs. At generation time, AdMuse coordinates skill routing, multi-candidate ideation and selection, and VLM-guided visual refinement: diverse skills propose alternative concepts, relative judgment selects the most promising direction, and vision-language feedback helps realize the selected intent in the final image. This design treats advertising creativity as a structured decision process rather than a single prompt-writing step. Experiments across diverse product and brand positionings show that AdMuse produces visually compelling advertisements that faithfully communicate product positioning.

</details>


### [168] [Video Generation Meets Agent Harness: Agentic Video Generation, Agentic World Modeling and Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40191)
*Liaoyuan Fan,Yuhao Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Hangyu Ran,Siyuan Hu,Weijia Mao,Rui Zhao,Weijia Wu,Guian Fang,Yanzhe Chen,Zeyu Zhu,Yuxin Jiang,Yiren Song,Yuchao Gu,Junchao Yi,Qi Hu,Dan Kong,Alex Jinpeng Wang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Mike Zheng Shou,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告从"agent harness（智能体运行框架）"这一交汇点切入，系统考察智能体视频生成、智能体世界建模、视频模型开发中的递归自我改进三个方向，分析其所需智能体能力（视听感知、长程规划、记忆、代码交互、反馈控制、技能复用、多智能体协作），并提出自主视频生产、终身记忆、可编程混合控制、自主模型开发、溯源、成本核算与评估等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成正从端到端合成转向由规划、工具使用与反馈驱动的智能体式生成，可实现连贯可控的多镜头视频；同时视频世界模型可作为动作条件化的交互环境。两者共享"围绕视频模型构建的可执行运行时"这一结构，但缺乏统一视角的组织分析，故需研究其交汇与相互促进关系。

Method: 以agent harness为分析框架（负责构造上下文、暴露工具、维护状态、路由反馈、管理预算），沿三条主线展开概念性综述与分析：(1) 智能体视频生成——harness协调脚本、镜头规划、素材管理、制作与剪辑，并讨论评估难题；(2) 智能体世界建模——harness中介观测、控制、执行与状态，讨论游戏引擎或智能体编写程序维护世界状态、视频模型渲染观测的混合设计，以及智能体与世界模型的互评与共进；(3) 视频模型开发中的递归自我改进（RSI），区分输出修订、harness状态更新、生成器参数更新、评估器更新及改进程序本身的变更，并分析其反馈机制。

Result: 提出三层结构化的分析框架，辨识三类系统间的共性与差异；归纳跨方向的七项关键智能体能力；刻画混合式世界状态与渲染分工的设计空间；建立RSI的分层改进谱系并说明各级改进如何回流至后续开发。

Conclusion: 未来挑战集中在自主视频生产、终身记忆、可编程混合控制、自主模型开发、溯源与成本核算，以及将视频质量与任务表现相耦合的评估体系；核心目标是使模型与harness获得持续、可验证的改进。

Abstract: Video generation is shifting from end-to-end synthesis toward agentic generation, where planning, tool use, and feedback enable coherent, controllable multi-shot videos. In parallel, video world models provide action-conditioned observations and predicted futures, serving as interactive environments for agents. We study their intersection through the agent harness: an executable runtime that constructs context, exposes tools, maintains state, routes feedback, and manages budgets around video models. This report examines three directions. First, agentic video generation, where harnesses coordinate scripting, shot planning, asset management, production, and editing, alongside the evaluation challenges of these systems. Second, agentic world modeling, where harnesses mediate observation, control, execution, and state. We discuss hybrid designs in which game engines or agent-written programs maintain world state while video models render observations, and how agents and world models can mutually evaluate and improve one another. Third, recursive self-improvement (RSI) in video model development, distinguishing output revisions, updates to harness state, generator parameters, and evaluators, and changes to the improvement procedure itself. We identify how these improvements feed back into subsequent development. Across these directions, we analyze essential agent capabilities, including audiovisual perception, long-horizon planning, memory, code-based interaction, feedback-guided control, skill reuse, and multi-agent coordination. We conclude with challenges in autonomous video production, lifelong memory, programmable hybrid control, autonomous model development, provenance, cost accounting, and evaluation linking video quality and task performance to sustained improvements in models and harnesses.

</details>


### [169] [Benchmarking color judgment in multimodal LLMs with CIE-computable ground truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40212)
*Shuwei Yue,Kaida Xiao,Zhihua Wang,Qiao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于CIE色度学框架的五任务基准，检验八种MLLM的图像质量"色彩判断"能力，发现模型擅长粗粒度二分类（AWB判断），但数值CCT估计与细粒度色差判断较弱，且色彩判断未可靠对齐CIE可计算的色差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM已被用作零样本图像质量评判者，但其色彩判断很少以色度学真值（colorimetric ground truth）进行验证，缺乏对"色彩感知是否校准"的实证检验。

Method: 构建五个任务的基准：AWB成对判断、肤色偏色方向、CCT估计、CIE ΔE2000成对排序、变化光源下的颜色命名；测试八个MLLM端点（4个Qwen3-VL变体、GLM-4.5V、GPT-5.4、Claude-Opus-4.7、Gemini-3.1-Pro）；在ColorChecker-24刺激上评估，并设置非ColorChecker对照实验以检验模板依赖性。

Result: 模型普遍能完成粗粒度二值AWB判断，但在数值CCT估计和部分细粒度色差判断上表现薄弱；ColorChecker-24上三个闭源前沿模型CCT MAE为715–1056 K，五个开源模型为1358–2133 K；非ColorChecker对照显示该优势部分依赖色卡模板。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM具备可用的色彩词汇与粗粒度视觉先验，但其色彩判断无法可靠校准到CIE可计算的色差，不能直接作为色度学意义上的可靠评判者。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are now used as zero-shot judges of image quality, but their color judgments are rarely tested with colorimetric ground truth. We present a five-task benchmark based on the CIE colorimetric framework: AWB pair judgment, skin-tone bias direction, CCT estimation, CIE Delta E 2000 pair ordering, and color naming under changing illuminants. We test eight MLLM endpoints, including four Qwen3-VL variants, GLM-4.5V, GPT-5.4, Claude-Opus-4.7, and Gemini-3.1-Pro. The models often solve coarse binary AWB judgments, but they remain weak on numeric CCT estimation and on some fine color-difference judgments. On ColorChecker-24 stimuli, the three closed-weight frontier models reach CCT MAE of 715-1056 K, compared with 1358-2133 K for the five open-weight models. A non-ColorChecker control shows that this advantage is partly template-dependent. The results suggest that current MLLMs have useful color words and coarse visual priors, but their color judgments are not reliably calibrated to CIE-computable color differences.

</details>


### [170] [TaskFrame: Connecting Task-Level Planning with Frame-Level Control for Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40276)
*Guangzhi Wang,Junfu Pu,Yiran Wang,Xingyilang Yin,Honghao Chen,Cheng Cheng,Lingen Li,Yinan Zhou,Jiarui Wu,Yinuo Jiang,Hongbo Jin,Xiaodong Cun,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层规划器-控制器框架 TaskFrame，将任务级规划与帧级键鼠控制解耦，在 Minecraft 中实现优于基线的闭环战斗成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用游戏智能体既需高层任务推理，又需在快速变化的视觉环境中低延迟动作控制，二者对模型的计算特性要求差异大，难以在单一模型中兼顾。

Method: 视觉语言任务规划器将高层目标分解为有序原子指令序列，用监督学习与依赖感知 GRPO 优化；以终止每条原子指令的特殊目标 token 的隐藏状态作为连续目标嵌入，缓存并在控制步间复用；因果 Transformer 控制器结合当前目标嵌入与近期观测，逐步预测离散键盘/鼠标按键与连续相机运动。训练分三阶段：规划器训练、控制器预训练、联合对齐。

Result: 指令分解能力与通用视觉语言模型相当；闭环评估中战斗成功率高于是所评估的基线。

Conclusion: 分层解耦的规划-控制架构能有效兼顾高层推理与低延迟控制，目标嵌入缓存与三阶段训练是关键设计。

Abstract: Building general game agents requires both high-level task reasoning and low-latency action control in rapidly changing visual environments. These capabilities favor models with substantially different computational profiles, making them difficult to realize within a single model. In this work, we present TaskFrame, a hierarchical planner-controller framework that connects task-level planning with frame-level keyboard-and-mouse control. A vision-language task planner decomposes a high-level goal into an ordered sequence of atomic instructions and is optimized through supervised learning and dependency-aware GRPO. The hidden states of special goal tokens that terminate each atomic instruction provide continuous goal embeddings that are cached and reused across control steps. A causal Transformer controller combines the embedding of the active goal with recent observations to predict discrete keyboard and mouse-button actions and continuous camera motion at every game step. We train the system in three stages: planner training, controller pretraining, and joint alignment. Experiments in Minecraft show that TaskFrame is competitive with general-purpose vision-language models on instruction decomposition, and that in closed-loop evaluation the agent achieves a higher combat success rate than the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [171] [LoRe-MIM: Lost-or-Replaced Masked Image Modeling for Self-Supervised Representation Learning in Whole-Body PET/CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40284)
*Amartya Bhattacharya,Bashirul Azam Biswas,Biratal Raj Wagle,Michael S. Leapman,Matthew E. Maeder,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoRe-MIM自监督框架，在6,531例全身PET/CT数据上通过"丢失或替换"掩码图像建模学习跨模态表征，在多项下游任务上超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT深度学习在病灶检测、治疗计划与疗效评估中潜力巨大，但专家标注耗时昂贵，成为模型鲁棒性与泛化性的瓶颈。自监督学习虽可缓解标注稀缺，却因大规模数据缺乏及跨模态互补表征学习困难，在全身PET/CT领域研究不足。

Method: 构建异构、多示踪剂、多机构的6,531例全身PET/CT队列；提出LoRe-MIM（Lost-Or-Replaced Masked Image Modelling）框架，采用创新的掩码策略：将图像区域置零（Lost）或替换为对应的互补模态图像（Replaced），迫使模型同时学习模态特异特征与跨模态关联。

Result: 在PET/CT多模态图像重建、patch级癌症/非癌症分类、全身病灶分割、标签高效训练与鲁棒性评估等下游任务中，LoRe-MIM均一致优于对应的SOTA基线。

Conclusion: LoRe-MIM可作为面向全身PET/CT分析的可扩展、可泛化的自监督学习框架，有效缓解标注稀缺问题并释放跨模态表征潜力。

Abstract: Deep learning models for whole-body positron emission tomography/computed tomography (PET/CT) hold significant clinical potential for disease detection, treatment planning, and response assessment. However, the time and cost associated with expert annotations remain major bottlenecks in developing robust and generalizable models. Although self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising approach to mitigating annotation scarcity, its application to whole-body PET/CT remains relatively underexplored due to the limited availability of large-scale datasets and the challenges associated with learning complementary cross-modal representations. To address these limitations, we curate a heterogeneous, multi-tracer, multi-institutional cohort comprising 6,531 whole-body PET/CT scans and propose LoRe-MIM (Lost-Or-Replaced Masked Image Modelling), a novel self-supervised framework designed to learn multimodal representations through masked image reconstruction. Specifically, LoRe-MIM introduces a masking strategy in which image regions are either replaced with zeros (Lost) or substituted with corresponding complementary imaging modality (Replaced), encouraging the model to capture both modality-specific characteristics and cross-modal relationships. We evaluate the learned representations across multiple downstream tasks, including multimodal PET/CT image reconstruction, patch-based classification into cancer vs. non-cancer, whole-body lesion segmentation, label-efficient training, and robustness assessment. Experimental results demonstrate that LoRe-MIM consistently outperforms the corresponding state-of-the-art baselines across these tasks, highlighting its potential as a scalable and generalizable self-supervised learning framework for whole-body PET/CT analysis.

</details>


### [172] [Self-training for cross-centre coronary vessel segmentation in X-ray angiography using pre-contrast frame subtraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40302)
*Danial Moafi,Aida Moafi,Mostafa Aryafar,Ashkan Sami,Maryam Aryafar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了无额外人工标注情况下，利用伪标签自训练提升冠脉血管分割模型在外部数据上泛化性能的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习冠脉分割模型在训练中心未覆盖的外部数据上性能下降；同时最大公开数据集ARCADE标签不完整（仅标注SYNTAX评分定义的血管段），限制了模型的监督信息与跨中心泛化能力。

Method: 伪标签有两个来源：在ARCADE上训练的nnU-Net预测结果，以及同一序列中造影前帧相减得到的对比度图（contrast map）。将nnU-Net预测沿对比度图扩展，仅在减影图中检出而预测未覆盖的像素赋予ignore标签并在损失中排除。共训练六个模型，在ARCADE及两个外部数据集（XCAD、XACV）上测试，并与基线及同轮数微调的对照模型比较；另开展第二轮自训练，并尝试以50张全标注图像训练的外部模型生成初始标签。

Result: 所有以基线预测初始化的首轮模型均优于基线和对照模型：基于XCAD数据池训练的模型宏平均Dice提升2.7分（95% CI 2.3–3.2），而对照模型下降0.6分；第二轮自训练无进一步提升。以50张全标注图像训练的外部模型生成初始标签时，在无任何XCAD标签的情况下XCAD Dice由0.623升至0.745，但XACV未改善，原因是主大血管被漏检。

Conclusion: 自训练带来的收益在很大程度上取决于生成初始伪标签所用模型的敏感度（召回能力）；伪标签质量比迭代轮数更关键。

Abstract: Deep learning models for coronary vessel segmentation in X-ray angiography often perform poorly on data from centres that were not represented in training. Apart from differences in equipment, the labels of the largest public dataset (ARCADE) are incomplete, as only the segments defined in the SYNTAX score are annotated. In this study, we evaluated whether self-training with pseudo-labels can improve performance on external data without additional manual annotation.
